## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_GOVERNMENT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_GOVERNMENT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S政府§!界面显示了关于我们帝国及其政府的有关信息。在这里可以看到我们的§S统治者§!以及相关的效果。\n\n另一个界面显示了我们帝国的§S人口统计§!数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

The §SGovernment§! screen presents us with information regarding our empire and its government. Here we can see our §SRuler§! and any related effects.\n\nA separate tab gives information on the §SDemographics§! of our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:9`

## TUTORIAL_TECH_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TECH_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

您可以在§S科技§!界面指定当前的科研投入方向。科技可以分为三个大类，每个大类一般有三个科技可供选择研究。\n\n这三个领域分别是£physics£物理学、£society£社会学和£engineering£工程学。\n\n研究§S科技§!；\n\n£dot_green£点击§S选择科技§!按钮\n£dot_green£选择一个选项\n\n指派一名§S首席科学官§!可以促进作出的研究进展。虽不是必须，但强烈推荐！此外，所任命领袖的§S特质§!也会带来某些增益。\n\n也可以在各个领域点击§S启用自动科研§!按钮来自动进行科研。\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£automate_research£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:9`

### English

The §STechnology§! screen is where we will be directing our research efforts. Technologies are categorized into three different fields, with each field typically having three available research options.\n\nThe available fields are £physics£ Physics, £society£ Society, and £engineering£ Engineering.\n\nTo research a §STechnology§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §SSelect Research§! button\n£dot_green£Choose from the available options\n\nAssigning a §SHead of Research§! speeds up progress and is strongly recommended. Their §STraits§! can also provide additional benefits.\n\nResearch can also be automated by clicking on the §SEnable Auto-Research§! button in each respective field.\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£automate_research£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:11`

## TUTORIAL_CONTACTSVIEW_EMPIRES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CONTACTSVIEW_EMPIRES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S通讯§!界面中我们可以看到所有我们遭遇过的§S帝国§!的信息。他们对我方的外交§S评价§!一目了然，我们也能方便快捷地展开§S外交§!。\n\n每个条目都展示着重要的详细信息，例如§S政体§!、§S思潮§!、以及§S帝国地位§!。\n\n我们可以在此访问§S谍报§!行动，查看与各个帝国现已签订的§S协议§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

In the §SContacts§! screen we see a detailed list of all the various §SEmpires§! we have encountered. Their §SOpinions§! of us are visible at a glance, and we can quickly and easily engage in §SDiplomacy§!.\n\nEach entry also shows important details such as §SGovernment Type§!, §SEthics§!, and §SEmpire Status§!.\n\nFrom here, we can also access §SEspionage§! operations and view existing §SAgreements§! with each empire.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:13`

## TUTORIAL_SITUATION_LOG_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_SITUATION_LOG_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S情报日志§!中详细列出了当前可用的§S特殊项目§!，£archaeology£§S考古遗址§!以及一些其它需要注意的事项。\n\n随着对宇宙探索的深入，新条目也会接连出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

The §SSituation Log§! displays a list of all currently available §SSpecial Projects§!, £archaeology£ §SArchaeology sites§!, and various other points of interest.\n\nNew items will likely appear as we begin exploring the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:15`

## TUTORIAL_SHIP_DESIGNER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_SHIP_DESIGNER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帝国现役的所有§S舰船§!都可以在此设计。随着科技发展您可以解锁新的§S舰船种类§!和§S部件§!\n\n要创建新的§S设计§!；\n\n£dot_green£点击§S新设计§!按钮\n£dot_green£选择§S船体规格§!\n£dot_green£点击§S没有区段§!按钮并选择一个§S区段§!\n\n然后，点击可用槽位，从可用的§S舰船部件§!中自由选用。\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£slot_component£\n\n\n保存之后，新设计在任何建有§S船坞§!的§S恒星基地§!都可以进行建造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

All the §SShips§! in use within our empire are designed here. New §SHull Sizes§! and §SComponents§! will be unlocked as we research new technologies.\n\nTo create a new §SDesign§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §SNew Design§! button\n£dot_green£ Choose §SHull Size§!\n£dot_green£ Click the §SNo Section§! button and select a §SSection§!\n\nNow, select freely from the available §SShip Components§! by clicking on the available slots.\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£slot_component£\n\n\nOnce saved, the new design will be buildable at any §SStarbase§! with a §SShipyard§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:17`

## TUTORIAL_EMPIREVIEW_PLANETS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIREVIEW_PLANETS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S星球与星域§!界面提供了我们所有殖民星球的概览，其£pops£§S人口§!，以及§S资源产出§!。\n\n随着帝国的发展，星球将可以被组成§S星域§!以便于管理。\n\n£vassal£§S附庸国§!可以通过现存的§S星域§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

The §SPlanets and Sectors§! screen gives an overview of all colonized worlds, their £pops£ §SPops§!, and §SResource Output§!.\n\nAs the empire grows, it can be organized into §SSectors§! to streamline management.\n\n£vassal£ §SVassals§! can also be created from existing §SSectors§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:19`

## TUTORIAL_MILITARY_FLEET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_MILITARY_FLEET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S军用舰队§!能够保护我们的帝国免受威胁……如果您有足够的野心的话，舰队所指即是帝国边疆。\n\n我们舰队的规模受到当前的§S海军容量§!，一开始非常有限，但有如下方式可以提升：\n\n£dot_green£研究新§S科技§!\n£dot_green£增加£pops£人口\n£dot_green£建造§S恒星基地锚地§!\n\n我们可以为一支舰队指派一位£commander£§S指挥官§!，不仅舰队的战斗力将会提升，还能从这位£leader£§S领袖§!身上得到其他的增益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

§SMilitary Fleets§! are used to protect our emerging empire from threats... or to expand our glorious rule through force of arms, if we so wish.\n\nThe size of our fleet is determined by our current §SNaval Capacity§!, which is  initially limited. It can, however, be increased by;\n\n£dot_green£ Researching new §STechnologies§!\n£dot_green£ Growing our £pops£ population\n£dot_green£ Building §SStarbase Anchorages§!\n\nTo lead our fleets, we can assign a £commander£ §SCommander§!, granting extra military power along with any other bonuses the £leader£ §SLeader§! provides.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:21`

## TUTORIAL_SCIENCE_SHIP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_SCIENCE_SHIP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!，用于§S调查§!例如£system£§S恒星系§!中的£planet£§S行星§!等天体。需要先对行星进行调查才能勘探出其上的§S资源§!。\n\n我们的母星系已经完成了调查，所以我们可以将努力放在其他地方。\n\n选择§S科研船§!，\n\n£dot_green£右键点击一个邻近的§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S调查恒星系§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

This is our £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!, which is used to §SSurvey§! astronomical objects such as £planet£ §SPlanets§! in a £system£ §SStar System§!. A planet needs to be surveyed in order to make its §SResources§! visible.\n\nOur home system has already been surveyed, so we should focus our efforts elsewhere.\n\nWith the §SScience Ship§! selected;\n\n£dot_green£ Right-click a neighboring §SSystem§!\n£dot_green£ Select §SSurvey System§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:23`

## TUTORIAL_CONSTRUCTION_SHIP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CONSTRUCTION_SHIP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们的£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，可以用于建设空间站。\n\n当调查完一个天体，例如一颗£planet£§S行星§!之后，我们可以指挥该舰船前去建设一座£science_ship£§S研究站§!或£mining_station£§S开采站§!来开发利用其上蕴含的资源。\n\n这些§S舰船§!也可以用来在无主的£system£§S恒星系§![GetBuildGrow]§S恒星基地§!，因此这种舰船对我们的扩张宏图至关重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

This is our £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!, which is used to construct space stations.\n\nWhen an astronomical object such as a £planet£ §SPlanet§! has been surveyed, we can order this ship to build a £science_ship£ §SResearch Station§! or a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! to exploit any resources it may have.\n\nThese §SShips§! are also used to [GetBuildGrow] §SStarbases§! in unclaimed £system£ §SSystems§!, making them critical to our expansion plans.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:25`

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_PLANET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_PLANET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S地表§!选项卡可用于总览£colony£§S殖民地§!的基础设施和状态。\n\n£building£§S区划与建筑§!部分在相应区划中显示已建好和待建造的建筑槽。\n\n£district£§S区划§!和£zone£区划特化提供£housing£§S住房§!、£job£§S岗位§!和资源。\n\n§S自动化§!开关让殖民地可以基于当前的关注焦点自行管理。\n\n窗口顶部的§S行星统计§!条显示£stability£§S稳定度§!、£crime£§S犯罪度§!、生效的修正以及指派的§S总督§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

The §SSurface§! tab provides an overview of the infrastructure and status of a £colony£ §SColony§!.\n\nThe £building£ §SDistricts and Buildings§! section shows constructed and available building slots in their respective Districts.\n\nThe £district£ §SDistricts§! and £zone£ District Specializations provide £housing£ §SHousing§!, £job£ §SJobs§!, and resources.\n\nThe §SAutomation§! toggle allows the planet to manage itself based on focus.\n\nThe §SColony Stats§! bar in the top section of the window displays £stability£ §SStability§!, £crime£ §SCrime§!, active modifiers, and the assigned §SGovernor§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:27`

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_TILES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_TILES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S经济§!选项卡中概述了§S劳动力§!和£job£§S岗位§!的大体情况。\n\n£planet£§S行星§!上的人口分为不同的§S阶层§!，点击后可以展开显示其中的不同§S岗位§!类型。£pops£§S人口§!会维持§S劳动力§!需求而自动填满整个阶层。\n\n§S岗位§!会消耗、产出各种资源。\n\n人口需要£housing£§S住房§!和£amenities£§S舒适度§!。\n\n无论何种基础设施短缺都会降低行星£happiness£§S幸福度§!，最终可能导致整体£stability£§S稳定度§!的降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

The §SEconomy§! tab provides an overview of §SWorkforce§! and £job£ §SJobs§!.\n\nThe £planet£ §SPlanet's§! population is organized into §SStrata§!, which can be expanded to reveal §SJob§! types. £pops£ §SPops§! will fill strata as needed to maintain the §SWorkforce§!.\n\n§SJobs§! consume and produce resources.\n\nPops require £housing£§SHousing§! and £amenities£ §SAmenities§!.\n\nA shortage of either reduces planet £happiness£ §SHappiness§!, which in turn affects the overall £stability£ §SStability§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:29`

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_COMBAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_COMBAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S陆军§!选项卡用于管理行星上的地面不对。这一界面也显示£planet£ §S行星§!遭受的§S荒废度§!，也就是受到了多少伤害。\n\n点击§S招募§!按钮可以在此培训新的§S陆军§!。\n\n§S防御不对§!由从事执法者或是[soldier.GetName]§S岗位§!£pops£ §S人口§!自动生产。这些£job£ §S岗位§!由星球首府，£building£ §S警署§!，以及£building£ §S要塞§!提供。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

The §SArmies§! tab manages all ground forces on the planet. It also tracks how much §SDevastation§!, or damage, the £planet£ §SPlanet§! has suffered.\n\nNew §SArmies§! are raised here, by clicking the §SRecruit§! button.\n\n§SDefense Armies§! are produced automatically by £pops£ §SPops§! working Enforcer or [soldier.GetName] §SJobs§!. These £job£ §SJobs§! are provided by the planetary capital, £building£ §SPrecinct Houses§!, and £building£ §SStrongholds§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:31`

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_SPACEPORT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_SPACEPORT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S太空港§!界面可以查看轨道上的舰队，[GetBuildGrow]舰船。军用舰船只能在建有§S船坞§!的§S恒星基地§!建造，但是工程船、科研船和§S殖民船§!能在这个界面建造。\n\n只要还有一个宜居£planet£§S星球§!，即使失去了所有的恒星基地，您也能重整旗鼓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:31`

### English

The §SSpaceport§! tab is where we view orbiting fleets and [GetBuildGrow] new ships. Military ships can only be constructed at a §SStarbase§! equipped with a §SShipyard§!, but Construction, Science and §SColony Ships§! are all built here.\n\nEven if we lose our last Starbase, we still have the means to expand and rebuild as long as we possess at least one inhabited £planet£ §SPlanet§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:33`

### English

Military Stations

## TUTORIAL_MILITARY_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_MILITARY_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S防御站§!是用来保护某些重要的£planet£§S行星§!与维持战略要道的静态防御，但它只能建设在国境线内或者有特殊通行权的地区。\n\n请注意不要[GetBuildGrow]过多军用空间站，它们和§S战舰§!一样都需要维护费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

§SMilitary Stations§! are static defenses that can be used to protect important £planet£ §SPlanets§! or strategic avenues of approach, but can only be built within our borders or territories we have special access to.\n\nTake care not to [GetBuildGrow] too many - like §SShips§!, their upkeep costs will be a drain on our economy.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:35`

## TUTORIAL_RESEARCH_STATION_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_RESEARCH_STATION_TITLE&lang=en)

教程：研究站

### English

Tutorial: Research Stations

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:36`

## TUTORIAL_RESEARCH_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_RESEARCH_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

天体的轨道上能够建造£science_ship£§S研究站§!，它可以收集£physics£§S物理§!、£engineering£§S工程§!、£society£§S社会§!研究点数。\n\n研究站只能建造在帝国境内，已殖民的£planet£§S行星§!无需额外建造研究站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

A £science_ship£ §SResearch Station§! built in orbit of a planetary body will gather its £physics£ §SPhysics§!, £engineering£ §SEngineering§! and £society£ §SSociety§! research data.\n\nThey can only be built within our borders, and they are not needed if the £planet£ §SPlanet§! has already been colonized.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:37`

Mining Stations

## TUTORIAL_MINING_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_MINING_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

£mining_station£§S开采站§!可以采集所环绕天体的£minerals£§S矿物§!和£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!。\n\n已殖民£planet£§S行星§!上的资源不需要§S开采站§!就可以使用。开采站只能建造在帝国境内。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

£mining_station£ §SMining Stations§! are used to extract the £minerals£ §SMinerals§! and £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§! of the objects they orbit.\n\n§SMining Stations§! are not needed to access the resources of colonized £planet£ §SPlanets§!, and can only be built within our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:39`

## TUTORIAL_TRANSPORT_SHIP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TRANSPORT_SHIP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

当§S陆军§!受命进入太空，就会自动登上§S运输§!舰。运输舰几乎没有自卫能力，所以必须受到保护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

When §SArmies§! are ordered into space, they automatically embark on these §STransport§! ships. Make sure to protect them, since they are quite defenseless!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:41`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§S外交§!界面，我们可以与其他文明进行交流。例如§S宣战§!、在我们的邻国建立§S间谍网§!，组建§S联盟§!、制定§S贸易协定§!等等。我们也能看到当前的§H情报等级§!。\n\n你与其他国家的§S关系§!将以五个级别显示出来，从恶劣到极佳。为了达成交易或者协议，必须拥有足够的关系级别。\n\n你的£envoy£§S特使§!可以被指派去其他国家来从事§S间谍§!行动以收集§S情报§!，达成绕开§S关系§!等级限制的需求，或是§S改善§!及§S损害关系§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

The §SDiplomacy§! screen is where we communicate with other civilizations. Here we can §SDeclare War§!, build §SSpy Networks§! on our neighbors, or establish §SAlliances§! and §STrade Deals§! with them. We can also see the current §HIntel level§!.\n\nYour §SRelations§! with the selected Empire are shown as one of five levels, ranging from 'Terrible' to 'Excellent'. In order to make a deal or agreement you must have a sufficient Relations level.\n\nYou can assign £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! to an Empire in order to §SSpy§! on them, gather §SIntel§!, bypass §SRelations§! requirements or §SImprove§! or §SHarm Relations§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:43`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_TRADE_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_TRADE_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：贸易

### English

Tutorial: Trade

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:44`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_TRADE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_TRADE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是§S贸易界面§!。我们可以与其他国家交易任意一种类型的§S资源§!，也可以是§S情报§!和§S外交支持§!。\n\n外交支持可以增强另一个国家在接受协议时的意愿程度。你同样可以在§H星海共同体§!的参议院投票期间请求外交支持来增加你的外交权重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:43`

### English

This is the §STrade screen§!. We can trade any type of §SResource§! with an Empire, as well as §SInformation§! and §SFavors§!.\n\nFavors can be spent to increase an Empire's willingness to accept an agreement. You can also call in Favors during a Vote on the Senate Floor in the §HGalactic Community§! to Boost your Diplomatic Weight.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:45`

### English

Federation Types

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_FEDERATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_FEDERATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S联邦类型§!决定了£federation£§S联邦§!发展的侧重点。每个联邦类型都有着独特的初始§S天赋和法律§!并且将会在获得经验值后§S解锁§!更多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

§SFederation Types§! dictate what the focus of the £federation£ §SFederation§! will be. Each Federation type has unique starting §SPerks and Laws§! and will §SUnlock§! more Perks as it gains XP.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:47`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_HIGHINTEL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_HIGHINTEL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经在这个国家获取了§S高情报等级§!，这样我们就有办法获得他们相当数量的信息。\n\n保持对该国高情报等级将会保证我们及时获取他们的§S思潮§!，§S国民理念§!，与其他国家的§S关系§!，以及他们相对我们的§S科技§!，§S经济§!和§S军事§!力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

We have reached a §Shigh Intel level§! with this Empire, and so have access to a lot more information about them.\n\nMaintaining High Intel with an Empire will ensure we have up-to-date information about their §SEthics§! and §SCivics§!, their §SRelations§! with other Empires, and their §STechnology§!, §SEconomy§! and §SMilitary§! power relative to us.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:49`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_LOWINTEL_TITLE&lang=en)

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_LOWINTEL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_LOWINTEL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在这个帝国的§S情报等级§!正在下降。我们有可能失去我们之前收集到的情报，情报随着时间流逝也会§S滞后§!。\n\n要提升§S情报等级§!；\n\n£dot_green£维持§S外交协议§!\n£dot_green£派遣£envoy£§S特使§!增进关系\n£dot_green£维持§S间谍网§!\n£dot_green£在其境内设立§S大使馆§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

Our §SIntel level§! with this Empire is dropping. We risk losing the information we have previously gathered about them, as Intel becomes §Sstale§! over time.\n\nTo increase §SIntel Level§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Maintain §SDiplomatic Agreements§!\n£dot_green£ Assign £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! to improve relations\n£dot_green£ Establish a §SSpy Network§!\n£dot_green£ Set up an §SEmbassy§! in their territory.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:51`

### 中文

有些£building£§S建筑§!会对相邻§S地块§!上的§S建筑§!产生§S毗邻效应§!，建设新建筑时，应当纳入考虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

Some £building£ §SBuildings§! may cause §SAdjacency Effects§! to other §SBuildings§! in neighboring §STiles§!. Let us take this into consideration when constructing new facilities.

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_BUDGET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_BUDGET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S预算§!选项卡显示帝国每月的£energy£能量币§S收入§!和§S支出§!，还可以审阅一系列影响经济状况的因素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

The §SBudget§! tab tallies our empire's monthly §SIncome§! and §SExpenses§! in £energy £Energy Credits. Here we can also track a variety of effects that influence our economy.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:55`

### English

Edicts

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_DECISIONS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_DECISIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以在这里颁布帝国§S法令§!，即通过花费一些资源来施行的操作，其效果影响整个帝国。\n\n法令每月消耗维护费，可以由§H$EDICT_FUND$§!来支出或是直接花费£unity£§Y凝聚力§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

Here empire-wide §SEdicts§!, practices that can be enforced by spending some of our resources, can be enacted.\n\nEdicts have a monthly upkeep cost, which can be covered by our §H$EDICT_FUND$§! or by spending £unity£ §YUnity§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:57`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_DEMOGRAPHICS_TITLE

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_DEMOGRAPHICS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_DEMOGRAPHICS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S人口统计§!选项卡显示帝国人口的组成及分布。\n\n此外还显示帝国£pops£ §S人口§!的主要§S特质§!，对于生产效率、£happiness£ §S幸福度§!等的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

The §SDemographics§! tab tracks the makeup and spread of our empire's populace.\n\nIt also shows dominant §STraits§! among our £pops£ §SPops§! as well as modifiers to productivity and £happiness£ §SHappiness§!, among other things.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:59`

### English

Tutorial: Leaders

## TUTORIAL_EMPIREVIEW_LEADERS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIREVIEW_LEADERS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S领袖§!界面将展示你所拥有£leader£§S领袖§!的总览，你可以在该界面对他们进行雇佣、解雇和管理。\n\n领袖的§S类别§!将决定他们能够担任何种职位，以及在§S内阁§!中所能扮演的角色。\n\n待雇佣的§S领袖§!将周期性刷新。能够拥有领袖的数量不是无上限的，一旦超出了当前的限制容量，将会提高维护费并减少获取的经验。为了避免遭受以上惩罚课程遣散闲置的领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

The §SLeaders§! screen provides an overview of our £leader£ §SLeaders§! and lets us hire, dismiss, and manage them.\n\nA Leader's §SClass§! determines their possible assignments across our empire, and what position on the §SCouncil§! they can undertake.\n\nAvailable §SLeaders§! refresh periodically, but since capacity is limited, exceeding it raises upkeep and slows experience gain. Dismiss redundant leaders to avoid these penalties.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:61`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIREVIEW_FACTIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S派系§!界面显示了人口政治倾向的概览。注意社会政治气氛的变化可能会产生新的§S派系§!。这里也显示了§S派系§!规模，对我们£pops£§S人口§!的吸引力，以及派系满意程度的细节。\n\n§S派系§!有自己的§S诉求§!，这对他们的§S赞同度§!有着很大的影响，而我们国家的政策与行动可能会惹恼或取悦这些团体，增加£unity£§S凝聚力§!的产出。\n\n§S派系§!的实力与其§S诉求§!的性质会随着状况发生改变，但记住§S人口§!的£happiness£§S幸福度§!会受到其所属§S派系§!的§S赞同度§!影响。\n\n我们也可以用§S管理派系§!来调整某个§S派系§!与国家之间的关系，甚至接纳某个强大§S派系§!的思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

The §SFactions§! screen gives an overview of the populace's political leanings. Be aware that shifts in the sociopolitical climate may give rise to §SFactions§!. Details on §SFaction§! sizes, attractiveness to our £pops£ §SPops§!, and how content they are may also be found here.\n\n§SFactions§! have §SIssues§! that are important to their overall §SApproval§!, and our policies and actions as a nation may displease or please these groupings, providing an increasing boost to £unity£ §SUnity§! generation.\n\nThe strength of §SFactions§! and the nature of their §SIssues§! can change along with our circumstances, but keep in mind that the £happiness£ §SHappiness§! of a §SPop§! can be affected by the §SApproval§! of the §SFaction§! it belongs to.\n\nWe can also §SManage Factions§! to manipulate the relationship between a §SFaction§! and the state, even going so far as embracing the ethos of a strong §SFaction§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:63`

## TUTORIAL_CREATESECTOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CREATESECTOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

要建立一个§S星域§!，首先选中一颗在§S边境区域§!的£planet£§S行星§!并按下创建星域按钮。这会以此行星作为新星域的§S首府§!，所有在六次超空间跳跃内的边境区域恒星系都会被添加到此星域。星域在启动自动模式并给予资源后可以自动进行行星£building£§S建筑物§!建造，极大减少了微观管理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

To establish a §SSector§!, first select a £planet£ §SPlanet§! in §SFrontier space§! and press the Create Sector button. This will make the planet the §SCapital§! of a new Sector and all frontier space systems within six hyperjumps will be added to it. Sectors may automate the construction of planetary £building£ §SBuildings§! on planets within them if this option is enabled and they are given resources, greatly reducing the need for micromanagement.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:65`

## TUTORIAL_CONTACTSVIEW_SPECIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CONTACTSVIEW_SPECIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S物种§!界面罗列了我们接触到的所有智能生命形式。可以点击§S切换物种§!按钮切换显示我国生活的物种或者星系中所有已知物种。\n\n如果我们拥有相关技术并且政策上允许，我们可以§S修饰§!物种特质或者§S提升§!未开智生物。也可以为所有我国£pops£§S人口§!§S设置默认权利§!或者针对我国某一物种§S设置权利§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

The §SSpecies§! screen lists all types of nominally intelligent beings we have encountered. We can §SToggle Species§! to display only citizens of our empire or all known denizens of the galaxy.\n\nAccess and technology permitting, we can §SModify§! the traits of species, §SUplift§! pre-sapient creatures, and §SSet Default Rights§! for all of our £pops£ §SPops§! or §SSet Rights§! per-species for our citizens here.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:67`

可以通过§S环境改造§!改变£planet£§S行星§!环境，令其更适合某个§S物种§!居住，或用于其他目的。

## TUTORIAL_GENEMODIFICATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GENEMODIFICATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以通过基因工程修饰物种的§S特质§!。\n\n根据选择的新特质，将会创建新的物种§S模板§!。在修饰该物种的£pops£§S人口§!的基因之前，先点击§S应用模板§!按钮创建新的§S特殊项目§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

We can genetically engineer this species, modifying their §STraits§!.\n\nThis will create a new species §STemplate§!, with the new traits you have selected. Before any £pops£ §SPops§! of this species are actually altered, you will have to click on the §SApply Template§! button which will create a new §SSpecial Project§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:71`

我们可以修改原始生物的§S特质§!，将其§S提升§!成为有智慧和生产力的我国公民。

现在的界面是§S恒星系预览§!。选定特定£system£§S恒星系§!，可以方便地查看£planet£§S§!行星、太空港、舰队。

We are now in §SSystem View§!, an up-close look at a particular £system£ §SStar System§!. Here we can visually locate £planet£ §SPlanets§!, stations and fleets with relative ease.

## TUTORIAL_WORMHOLE_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_WORMHOLE_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S虫洞站§!建造在£system£§S星系§!的外围，可以将时空中的微小裂痕转化成通向其他星系的双向通道。真是匠心独具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

§SWormhole stations§! are built on the outer rim of a £system£ §SSystem§!, molding minute tears in space-time into two-way portals to other stars. Ingenious.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:77`

## TUTORIAL_OBSERVATION_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_OBSERVATION_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S观测站§!驻扎着一队£scientist£§S科学家§!，对所环绕£planet£§S行星上的§!§S前超光速§!物种进行观察研究。\n\n取决于帝国的§S思潮§!和科技，你可以选择被动观测、文化影响、科技启蒙甚至进行绑架或渗透。\n\n这些选项会影响文明的发展进程，以及对你帝国的看法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

§SObservation Posts§! house teams of £scientist£ §SScientists§! conducting research and remotely observing §Spre-FTL§! species on the £planet£ §SPlanets§! they orbit.\n\nDepending on your empire's §SEthics§! and technology, you can choose between passive observation, cultural influence, technological enlightenment, or even abduction and infiltration.\n\nThese choices affect how the civilization evolves, and how they view your empire.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:79`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_EDICTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些是星球§S决议§!，即通过花费一些资源来实施的临时规定或命令，其效果只影响实施决议的星球。\n\n随着新科技的研发与遭遇新的状况，新的§S决议§!也会随之解锁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

These are planet-specific §SDecisions§!, orders or practices that can be temporarily enforced by spending some of our resources.\n\nNew §SDecisions§! may become available as we research new technologies and encounter new situations.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:83`

### 中文

§S胜利§!界面列出了我们需要努力达成的§S特定条件§!，以便确保帝国在星系中其他智慧生命的环伺之下可以长治久安。

Tutorial: Starbases

## TUTORIAL_OUTPOST_STATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_OUTPOST_STATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们星系的§S恒星基地§!。这些可升级的空间站象征着星系的所有权，并且通常负责生产各种§S舰船§!。\n\n我们可以通过在邻近恒星系[GetBuildingGrowing]§S恒星基地§!来拓展边界。\n\n战斗中攻击敌方§S恒星基地§!即可占领。\n\n§S恒星基地§!可以通过各种§S模块§!和£building£§S建筑§!进行功能特化，并且通常也是星系抵御敌方舰队的最后一道防线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

This is our system's §SStarbase§!. These upgradable stations mark star system ownership and are most often tasked with producing all types of §SShips§!.\n\nWe expand our borders by [GetBuildingGrowing] §SStarbases§! in nearby systems.\n\nEnemy §SStarbases§! can be captured by attacking them.\n\n§SStarbases§! can be specialized into a variety of functions through §SModules§! and £building£ §SBuildings§!. They also serve as the last line of defense against enemy fleets in a system.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:89`

## TUTORIAL_GALAXY_MAP_TITLE

### English

## TUTORIAL_GALAXY_MAP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALAXY_MAP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S星系地图§!显示了广阔的§S未知区域§!，我方边界的范围，以及所有已建立通讯的外星文明的领地范围。\n\n点击右下方菜单栏的§S详细地图§!按键，即可切换§S资源§!和§S探测范围§!的显示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

The §SGalaxy Map§! displays vast swaths of §SUncharted Space§!, the limits of our borders, and the territories of any alien civilizations we may have established communications with.\n\nWe can click the §SDetails Map Mode§! button in the bottom-right menu bar to toggle §SResources§! and §SSensor Ranges§! displays.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:91`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_WAR_GOAL_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

战争目标

### English

War Goal

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:92`

## TUTORIAL_WAR_GOAL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_WAR_GOAL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在宣战时，我们也需要对敌方和盟友宣布自己的意图。这些§S战争目标§!决定了我们在和谈时可以提出怎样的条件，从吞并有宣称的£system£§S恒星系§!到建立附庸均可成为您的目标。\n\n宣布目标后敌方有机会做出反应——他们可能会立即投降，亦或是讨价还价并提出休战，或保卫自身的利益直至最后一个雄性，雌性、菌类孢子或是半智慧的食草动物。弄清您敌人的心理底线是乐趣的一部分！\n\n在列表中能够选择§S战争目标§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

When declaring war we also need to declare our intention, to both our enemy and any allies. This §SWar Goal§! determines what kind of demands we can press during peace negotiations, ranging from the ceding of claimed £system£ §SSystems§! to vassalization.\n\nPresenting this goal gives the enemy an opportunity to react appropriately - they might give up immediately, present a counter-offer and truce, or defend their interests to the last man, woman, fungal spore or half-sapient herbivore. Finding out is part of the fun!\n\nTo declare a §SWar Goal§!, select it in the list.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:93`

### English

War Overview

## TUTORIAL_WAROVERVIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_WAROVERVIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个界面是我们当前正在进行的战争的概览。双方的§S厌战度§!由战争进程决定，厌战度表示交战方对和平协议的接受意愿。\n\n如果一方的厌战度达到100%，另一方可以强制其接受§S维持现状§!的协议。战争中已经进行过的战斗都可以在这里查看到记录。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

This window presents an overview of our current war. Each side suffers §SWar Exhaustion§! depending on how the war is progressing, which determines how willing they are to accept a peace agreement.\n\nIf War Exhaustion reaches 100% for one side, the other side can enforce a §SStatus Quo§! agreement. The results of all engagements that have so far been fought in the war can also be tracked here.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:95`

## TUTORIAL_STRATEGIC_RESOURCES_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_STRATEGIC_RESOURCES_TITLE&lang=en)

战略资源

### English

Strategic Resources

## TUTORIAL_STRATEGIC_RESOURCES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_STRATEGIC_RESOURCES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H战略资源§!界面将我们现有的£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!和它们的效果进行分类。\n\n许多§S战略资源§!需要特定的§S科技§!才能进行开采和利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

The §HStrategic Resources§! screen catalogs our available £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§! and their effects.\n\nMany §SStrategic Resources§! require specific §STechnologies§! to be researched before they can be mined and utilized.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:97`

## TUTORIAL_EXPANSION_PLANNER_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EXPANSION_PLANNER_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：扩张规划器

### English

Tutorial: Expansion Planner

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:98`

## TUTORIAL_EXPANSION_PLANNER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EXPANSION_PLANNER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H扩张规划§!界面我们可以找到附近能够殖民的§S宜居§!£planet£§S星球§!。我们可以[GetBuildGrow]£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!并从这个界面直接向它们下达命令。\n\n在进行殖民前必须先勘探行星并研究在星球上发现的所有异常现象。\n\n您还需要在该§S行星§!所属的£system£§S恒星系§!中建造§S恒星基地§!来将其置于国境线内。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

In the §HExpansion Planner§! we find nearby §SHabitable§! £planet£ §SPlanets§! that may present opportunities for colonization. We can [GetBuildGrow] £colony_ship£ §SColony Ships§! and issue them orders directly from this screen.\n\nPlanets must be surveyed and any anomalies found on the planet must be researched before the planet can be colonized.\n\nA §SStarbase§! must also have been constructed in the §SPlanet's§! £system£ §SSystem§!, so that it lies within our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:99`

## TUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H传统§!显示我们国家可用的§S传统树§!。\n\n§S传统树§!首先必须被§S采纳§!，然后才可以解锁其中的£traditions£§S传统§!。\n\n§S采纳§!一个§S传统树§!或解锁一个§S传统§!需要消耗一定的£unity£§H凝聚力§!——而且随着更多§S传统§!的解锁与帝国的不断成长，消耗量也会随之增加。\n\n要采纳§S传统树§!；\n\n£dot_green£点击§S选择§!按钮\n£dot_green£在要选择的传统树上点击§S采纳§!\n\n将鼠标悬停在§S传统树§!上可以查看可用§S传统§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

The §HTraditions§! screen displays the §STradition Trees§! available to our empire.\n\nA §STradition Tree§! must first be §SAdopted§! before any £traditions£ §STraditions§! within it can be unlocked.\n\n§SAdopting§! a §STradition tree§! or unlocking a specific §STradition§! costs a certain amount of £unity£ §HUnity§! - and the price goes up as we unlock §STraditions§! and our empire grows.\n\nTo adopt a §STradition Tree§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §SSelect§! button\n£dot_green£ Click the §SAdopt§! button on the preferred tree\n\nAvailable §STraditions§! can be viewed by hovering over the §STradition Trees§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:101`

## TUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_SELECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_SELECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这里显示我们国家全部可用的§H传统树§!。你可以在这里检视每个传统树的全部£traditions£§H传统§!、§H采纳§!和§H完成效果§!。\n\n我推荐你从这些§H传统树§!开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

This screen displays all the §HTradition trees§! available to our empire. Here you can check all the £traditions£ §HTraditions§! in each tree as well as the §Hadoption§! and §Hfinisher effects§!.\n\nI recommended some of the §HTradition trees§! for you to start.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:103`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_NEW_TITLE&lang=en)

新传统或传统树

New Tree or Tradition

## TUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_NEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_TRADITIONS_NEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们国家有足够的£unity£§H凝聚力§!时，你可以§H选择§!一个§H新传统树§!或者你之前已选的传统树的新£traditions£§H传统§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

When our empire has enough £unity£ §HUnity§!, you can either §HSelect§! a §Hnew tree§! or a £traditions£ §HTradition§! in your previously chosen tree.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:105`

## TUTORIAL_ASCENSION_PERKS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASCENSION_PERKS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H飞升§!列表显示了我们有可能解锁的§S飞升天赋§!。某些§S飞升§!可能会有特殊要求，比如特定§S科技§!或是已解锁一定数量的§S飞升槽§!。\n\n在我们采纳一个§S传统树§!中的所有£traditions£§H传统§!后会解锁一个§H飞升槽§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

The §HAscension Perks§! list shows the §SAscension Perks§! that we can potentially unlock. Some §SAscension Perks§! may have special requirements like §STechnologies§! or having a certain number of already-unlocked §SAscension Perk Slots§!.\n\nAn §HAscension Perk Slot§! is unlocked whenever we adopt all £traditions£ §HTraditions§! in one of the §STradition trees§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:107`

## TUTORIAL_SPECIES_RIGHTS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_SPECIES_RIGHTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H物种权利§!允许我们设置某个特定物种的§S公民权§!、§S生活标准§!以及一些其他事项。\n\n设置的§S默认权利§!将适用于所有没有特定§S物种权利§!的£pops£§S人口§!——比如移民，难民，或是其他刚来到我们国家的新居民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

The §HSpecies Rights§! list allows us to customize the §SCitizenship§! and §SLiving Standards§! of a certain species, amongst other things.\n\nWhen customizing §SDefault Rights§!, the rights apply to all £pops£ §SPops§! that have not had specific §SSpecies Rights§! set - such as migrants, refugees, or other newcomers to our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:109`

## TUTORIAL_FLEET_MANAGER_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FLEET_MANAGER_TITLE&lang=en)

### English

## TUTORIAL_FLEET_MANAGER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FLEET_MANAGER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§S舰队管理§!界面，可以创造舰队模板。舰队规模增长后，这些设定好的模板能够快捷便利的补充受损舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

In the §SFleet Manager§!, we can create templates for our military fleets. As our fleets grow in size, these templates make it easier to quickly order replacements for any losses they may have sustained.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:111`

### English

Tutorial: Claims

## TUTORIAL_CLAIMS_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CLAIMS_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§S宣称§!界面，可以花费£influence£§S影响力§!获得他国拥有的£system£§S恒星系§!的宣称。\n\n对他国§S恒星系§!拥有宣称能够获得§S宣战理由§!，允许您以征服这个恒星系为目标对他国宣战。\n\n如果战争获胜方多个盟友对同一个§S恒星系§!均有宣称，拥有最高§S宣称§!等级的国家会成为战争结束后这个恒星系的所有者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

Through the §SClaims§! interface, we can spend £influence£ §SInfluence§! to lay claim to £system£ §SStar Systems§! owned by others.\n\nHaving a claim on a §SSystem§! gives us a valid §SCasus Belli§! against the owner, allowing us to declare war for the purpose of conquering it.\n\nIn wars involving multiple allies, the empire with the highest §SClaim§! on a §SSystem§! will be the one gaining ownership upon the successful conclusion of the war.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:113`

## TUTORIAL_ANOMALIES_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ANOMALIES_TITLE&lang=en)

### English

Tutorial: Anomalies

## TUTORIAL_ANOMALIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ANOMALIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S异常现象§!界面列出了所有我们遇到的且尚未调查的§S异常现象§!及其位置，可以派遣有£scientist£§S科学官§!指挥的£science_ship£§S科研船§!进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

The §SAnomalies§! screen lists all unresearched §SAnomalies§! we have encountered, along with their locations. These can be investigated using a £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! with an assigned £scientist£ §SScientist§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:115`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_MARKET_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_MARKET_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：国内市场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Tutorial: Internal Market

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:116`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_MARKET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_MARKET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以通过£market£§S市场§!界面买卖资源。市场一开始是纯粹的国内市场，但在足够多帝国互相建立联系之后，也许会建立起某种§S泛星系贸易市场§!。\n\n资源在这里以£trade£§S贸易额§!为通货单位买卖。每一笔买卖成交都会影响价格：买入升高、卖出降低。\n\n§S自动贸易§!可以按月为单位设置执行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Via the £market£ §SMarket§! screen we can buy and sell resources. At first this is just an internal market, but if enough empires establish contact, they may form a §SGalactic Market§!.\n\nResources can be bought and sold here using your £trade£ §STrade§! Stockpile, which acts as currency. Prices change with each transaction: buying increases the price, while selling decreases it.\n\n§SAutomatic Trade§! deals can also be set up on a monthly basis.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:117`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_SLAVE_MARKET_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_SLAVE_MARKET_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：奴隶市场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

Tutorial: Slave Market

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:118`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_SLAVE_MARKET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_SLAVE_MARKET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

有钱能使外星人推磨。在§S奴隶市场§!，£pops£§S人口§!的买卖和其他的商品别无二致，他们的命运在星间颠沛流离。购买成功后，新的§S人口§!就会自动送货到所选的£planet£§S行星§!上，不必担心运输的问题。这个市场是包含这项服务的……当然要钱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Everything has a price, including people. On the §SSlave Market§!, £pops£ §SPops§! are bought and sold like commodities, their destinies rerouted across the stars. Once acquired, your new §SPops§! will be automatically delivered to the £planet£ §SPlanet§! of your choosing, no need to worry about logistics. The market takes care of that... for a price.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:119`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_POLICIES_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_POLICIES_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：政策

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Tutorial: Policies

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:122`

## TUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_POLICIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GOVERNMENTVIEW_POLICIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S政策§!界面涵盖了政府的§S政策§!，对我们帝国的运转有着广泛的影响。\n\n在某项具体§S政策§!方面，通常会有几种可以采取的立场，我们的选择会因为科技水平和§S思潮§!而有所限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

The §SPolicies§! tab covers government §SPolicies§!, which have wide-ranging effects on how our empire is run.\n\nThere are often a few possible stances to take for any given §SPolicy§!, though we may be limited by technology as well as our §SEthos§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:123`

## TRADE_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATRADE_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们的帝国发展壮大，我们£planet£§S行星§!之间的§S贸易§!在经济中占据的地位将会越来越重要。配备§S贸易中心§!的§S恒星基地§!将在其£system£§S恒星系§!内收集§S贸易额§!，并送往§S首都§!转换成£energy£§S能源币§!。\n\n如果§S恒星基地§!有更多的§S贸易中心§!，就可以从相邻的星系中收集§S贸易额§!。富裕的贸易路线可能会吸引§S海盗§!，不过他们可以由§S巡逻§!的舰队，以及§S恒星基地§!上的§S防御模块§!进行压制。§S机库模块§!尤为有效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

As our empire grows, §STrade§! between our £planet£ §SPlanets§! will become an increasingly large part of our economy. §SStarbases§! with §STrade Hubs§! collect §STrade§! within their £system£ §SSystem§! to be routed to the §SCapital§! for conversion to £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§!.\n\nMore §STrade Hubs§! means a §SStarbase§! can collect §STrade§! from adjacent systems as well. Rich Trade Routes may attract §SPirates§!, though they can be countered by Fleets on §SPatrol§!, and by §SDefense Modules§! on §SStarbases§!. §SHangar Modules§! are particularly effective for this.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:124`

## PLANETVIEW_CORPORATE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APLANETVIEW_CORPORATE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

如果已经签署了相关的协议，企业界面可让§S寰宇企业§!在其它帝国的£planet£§S行星§!上建立分部。建成之后，可以在这里查看并升级这些设施。尽管发生了第23周期的那些事件，但我们都知道，大企业会全心全意为顾客着想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The corporate tab allows §SMegacorporation§! to build Branch offices on the £planet£ §SPlanets§! of other Empires, provided they have signed the necessary paperwork. Once built, they can view and upgrade their facilities here as well. As we know, large Corporations wish only the best for their customers, events of the 23rd cycle notwithstanding.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:125`

## TUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是§H联邦界面§!。我们的£federation£§S联邦§!会在成员国保持良好§S团结度§!的情况下变得更为强大。新成员国加入我们的联邦时会损失一些团结度。\n\n向联邦指派£envoy£§S特使§!会提升团结度。\n\n随着我们的联邦更为强大，我们也会不断§S解锁增益§!以使我们在更高水平上互利共赢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

This is the §HFederation screen§!. Our £federation£ §SFederation§! will grow stronger while members maintain good §SCohesion§!. When a new member joins our Federation, some Cohesion is lost.\n\nAssigning £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! to the Federation will improve Cohesion.\n\nAs our Federation grows stronger we §SUnlock Perks§! that increase our mutual benefits.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:127`

## TUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_ENVOYS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_ENVOYS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

向我们的£federation£§S联邦§!添派£envoy£§S特使§!有助于提升§S团结度§!。要注意的是你拥有的特使数量是有限的。被指派到联邦的特使可以随后重新指派到其他§S外交行动§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

Adding £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! to our £federation£ §SFederation§! will help improve §SCohesion§!. Keep in mind that you are limited with how many Envoys you have. Envoys that are assigned to Federations can later be re-assigned to other §SDiplomatic Actions§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:129`

## TUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_MEMBERS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_MEMBERS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这里是§H联邦成员国§!界面。在此我们可以看到我们的§S成员国§!在多领域的实力和它们各自指派了多少£envoy£§S特使§!。这里也会显示§S思潮§!间的冲突，这会导致§S团结度§!损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

This is the §HFederation Members§! screen. Here we can see how powerful our §SMembers§! are in various areas, how many £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! they are contributing, and which of their §SEthic§! are conflicting and contributing to a loss of §SCohesion§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:131`

## TUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_NAVY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_NAVY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H联邦海军§!的建造依赖于各成员国放弃的§S海军容量§!，并听命于§S主席国§!。成员国均可建造£federation£§S联邦§!海军舰船，为联邦舰队做贡献。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

The §HFederation Navy§! is built from §SNavy Capacity§! given up by all Members, and is controlled by the §SPresident§!. Members can all build £federation£ §SFederation§! Navy ships to contribute to the Fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:133`

## TUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_LAWS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FEDERATION_VIEW_LAWS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

任意成员国均可提议对§H联邦法律§!进行变更。如果一条法律在£federation£§S联邦§!未能通过，则联邦会受到§S团结度惩罚§!。\n\n某些法律默认锁定，必须提升§S集权程度§!方能解锁。联邦必须先§S升级§!才能加强集权程度。\n\n注意，随着集权程度的加强，团结度将会受损。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

Changes to §HFederation Laws§! can be proposed by any member. If the £federation£ §SFederation§! fails to pass a Law, the Federation will suffer a §SCohesion Penality§!.\n\nSome Laws are Locked and require increasing the §SCentralization§! to unlock them. The Federation must first §SLevel Up§! to gain access to a Higher Centralization.\n\nBe cautious though, as the Centralization is increased, Cohesion will suffer.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:135`

### English

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎来到§H星海共同体§!，来自全星系的外交官汇聚一堂，以对§S议案§!投票的方式决定星系法。\n\n你可以支持§S队列§!中的一项§S已提出议案§!，这会为其增加等同于你的§S外交权重§!的支持度。\n\n队列中有着最高支持度的决议将在会期被提交给§S参议院§!。随后参议院会进行投票，决定其是否能成为§S星系法§!。\n\n要注意§S星系焦点§!，它会为社群的共同目标指明方向。\n\n最后，你可以这一界面看到所有成员国和可能因决议生效的所有§S星系修正§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Welcome to the §HGalactic Community§!, where diplomats from around the galaxy meet to decide galactic law by voting on §SResolutions§!.\n\nYou can support §SProposed Resolutions§! from the §SQueue§!, adding your §SDiplomatic Weight§! to their total support.\n\nThe resolution with the most support in the queue will move onto the §SSenate Floor§! once it is in session, where it will be voted on to determine whether it becomes §SGalactic Law§!.\n\nTake note of the §SGalactic Focus§!, as it sets a common goal for the community.\n\nLastly, you can see all member empires and any §SGalactic Modifiers§! currently in effect from resolutions on this screen.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:137`

### English

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_ENVOYS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_ENVOYS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

£envoy£§S特使§!可以被指派到星海共同体来提升你的§S外交权重§!。指派到星海共同体的特使随后也可以被重新指派到其他§S外交行动§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

£envoy£ §SEnvoys§! can be assigned to the Galactic Community to increase your §SDiplomatic Weight§!. Envoys that are assigned to the Galactic Community can later be re-assigned to other §SDiplomatic Actions§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:139`

### English

Tutorial: Senate Floor

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:140`

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_SENATE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_SENATE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H参议院§!是星海共同体决定§S队列§!中最高支持度的议案能否通过的场所。作为成员国，你可以投：§S赞成票，反对票或弃权票§!。如果决议通过，其将成为§S星系法§!并影响到整个星海共同体的所有成员。如果被否决了则在一定时间内无法再提出。\n\n没有投弃权票的帝国将会§S提升§!那些与其投票相同的帝国的评价，降低与之投票相反的帝国的评价。\n\n如果你从其他帝国获得了§S外交支持§!，你可以在此使用它们来增加你在§S参议院§!投票中的§S外交权重§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

The §HSenate Floor§! is where the galactic community votes to pass or defeat the resolution from the §SQueue§! that had the most support. As a member, you can vote: §SYes, No, or Abstain§!. If the resolution passes, it becomes §SGalactic Law§! and affects all members of the galactic community. If it is defeated, it cannot be proposed again for some time.\n\nEmpires that don't abstain will gain an §SOpinion Increase§! with those who voted the same way, and a decrease with those who voted against them.\n\nIf you have gained diplomatic §SFavors§! from other empires, you may use them here to boost your §SDiplomatic Weight§! in the §SSenate Floor§! vote.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:141`

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_RESOLUTIONS_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_RESOLUTIONS_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：议案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

Tutorial: Resolutions

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:142`

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_RESOLUTIONS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_RESOLUTIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H议案§!界面显示了目前生效的议案列表。你可以从议案链中选择并§S提议一项新议案§!，这会消耗£influence£影响力。\n\n你只可提出议案链中第一项未生效的§S议案§!，在进行激进的改革前必须先打下基础，即使如此也不是每个人都会支持。\n\n如果§S议案§!通过，议案链中的下一项议案在几年内将无法被提议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

The §HResolutions§! screen is a list of the currently enacted resolutions. You may §SPropose a New Resolution§! by selecting it from a Resolution chain, which will cost you £influence£ Influence.\n\nYou may only propose the first un-enacted §SResolution§! in a chain. Each step lays the groundwork for more radical changes, though support is not guaranteed.\n\nIf a §SResolution§! passes, the next one in the chain cannot be proposed for several years.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:143`

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_COUNCIL_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_COUNCIL_TITLE&lang=en)

星系理事会

### English

The Galactic Council

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:144`

## TUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_COUNCIL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_GALACTIC_COMMUNITY_COUNCIL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H星系理事会§!已经成立。一场§S选举§!将会开始，拥有最高外交权重的各成员国将得到§S议会席位§!。议会成员国的外交权重将会提高，并可以以§S紧急决议§!的形式直接向参议院提交提议。\n\n§S议会否决权§!可以在通过加强议会的决议后解锁。议会成员也可以使用§S特殊决议§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

The §HGalactic Council§! has been established. An §SElection§! will begin, and the members with the most diplomatic weight will gain §SCouncil Positions§!. Council members have an increased Diplomatic Weight and can push a Proposal to the Senate Floor as an §SEmergency Resolution§!.\n\nThe §SCouncil Veto§! ability can be unlocked by passing resolutions to strengthen the Council. Council members also have access to §SSpecial Resolutions§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:145`

## TUTORIAL_EMPIRE_SPRAWL_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIRE_SPRAWL_TITLE&lang=en)

### English

## TUTORIAL_EMPIRE_SPRAWL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIRE_SPRAWL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

£empire_sprawl£§H$EMPIRE_SIZE$§!是量化衡量帝国所控制的领土，其依据包括控制的£system£§S恒星系§!、£colony£§S殖民地§!、£district£§S区划§!及总人口的多少。\n\n帝国规模过大会对帝国的§S行政管理能力§!形成压力，并将提高§S科技§!、£traditions£§S传统§!、£leader£§S领袖§!以及§S法令§!的花费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

£empire_sprawl£ §H$EMPIRE_SIZE$§! is a measure of the territorial expanse of your empire in the form of controlled £system£ §SSystems§!, £colony£ §SColonies§!, £district£ §SDistricts§!, and Total Population.\n\nA high Empire Size is a strain on your empire's §SAdministrative Capacity§!, and will increase the cost of §STechnologies§!, £traditions£ §STraditions§!, £leader£ §SLeaders§!, and §SEdicts§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:147`

## TUTORIAL_FIRSTCONTACT_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_FIRSTCONTACT_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们同一个新的实体进行了接触——可能是§S太空生物§!，或者另一个§S帝国§!。我们可以通过进行§H第一次接触§!研究它们的更多细节。\n\n我们需要指派一名£envoy£§S特使§!负责这项任务。他们会通过工作同这个实体建立沟通，并破解他们可能使用的语言。\n\n要指派§S特使§!；\n\n£dot_green£在§S第一次接触§!窗口点击头像\n£dot_green£选择一位可用§S特使§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

We have come into contact with a new entity - perhaps a §Sspace-dwelling creature§!, or another §SEmpire§!. We can learn more about them by making §HFirst Contact§!.\n\nWe will need to assign an £envoy£ §SEnvoy§! to this mission. They will work to establish communications with the entity, and decipher any language they may use.\n\nTo assign an §SEnvoy§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click on the portrait in the §SFirst Contact§! window\n£dot_green£ Select one of the available §SEnvoys§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:149`

### English

Tutorial: Become the Crisis

## TUTORIAL_CRISIS_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CRISIS_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正处在§S化身天灾§!的伟正之路上。这条道路能够解锁特殊的加成，这会增强我们的实力，让我们成为一支对整个星系更加有威胁的势力。\n\n§S天灾§!界面显示我们的目标——执行具体的行动来获取§S[GetCrisisResource]§!。获取[GetCrisisResource]能解锁§S天灾等级§!和§S增益§!，帮助我们向整个星系释放自己全部的潜力。\n\n随着我们的帝国对周边国家愈发有威胁性，星海共同体可能会选择联合在一起，宣布我们为§S星系的威胁§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

We are now on our way to §SBecoming the Crisis§!. This path unlocks special bonuses, which will empower us to become a more threatening force within the galaxy.\n\nThe §SCrisis§! screen displays our objectives - specific actions we can perform in order to gain §S[GetCrisisResource]§!. Earning [GetCrisisResource] unlocks the §SCrisis Levels§! and §SPerks§! that we need in order to unleash our full potential upon the galaxy.\n\nAs our Empire becomes more threatening to those around us, the Galactic Community may choose to unite and declare us a §SThreat to the Galaxy§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:151`

## TUTORIAL_CRISIS_VIEW_LEVEL1_TITLE

### English

Tutorial: Crisis Levels

## TUTORIAL_CRISIS_VIEW_LEVEL1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_CRISIS_VIEW_LEVEL1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们向§S化身天灾§!的道路逐渐迈进，并达成特定的要求，我们能够解锁全新的天灾等级。每一级都会带来不同的加成，比如§S[GetCrisisResource]增益§!和特殊的§S舰船§!。\n\n一旦我们达到§S等级5§!——或者被星海共同体宣布为危机——我们就惹恼了整个星系。彼时我们也将同星海共同体进入§S全面战争§!状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

As we advance along the §SBecome the Crisis§! path and reach specific requirements, we will unlock new levels. Each comes with different bonuses, such as §S[GetCrisisResource] Perks§! and special §Sships§!.\n\nOnce we reach §SLevel 5§! - or else are declared a Crisis by the Galactic Community - we will have antagonized the whole galaxy. At that time we will also enter into §STotal War§! with the Galactic Community.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:153`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_BUILDING_SPYNETWORK_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_BUILDING_SPYNETWORK_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：间谍网

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

Tutorial: Spy Networks

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:154`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_BUILDING_SPYNETWORK_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_VIEW_BUILDING_SPYNETWORK_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我方的一名£envoy£§S特使§!正在这个帝国中构建§S间谍网§!。\n\n基于当前§S间谍网值§!及目标£empire_sprawl£§S帝国规模§!，间谍网的§S渗透等级§!会随时间提升。在间谍网发展的同时，我方也会得到关于目标的更多§S情报§!。我方也可以在此经由§S谍报页面§!发起隐秘§S行动§!。\n\n如果没有为间谍网指派特使或是其存在因隐秘§S行动§!而暴露，间谍网会开始§P衰落§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

One of our £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! is now building a §SSpy Network§! in this Empire.\n\nThe §SInfiltration level§! of the Spy Network will increase over time, depending on the current §SSpy Network value§! and the target's £empire_sprawl£ §SEmpire Size§!. As the Spy Network grows, we will gain more §SIntel§! about the target. We will also be able to launch covert §SOperations§! here, via the §SEspionage tab§!.\n\nA Spy Network will start to §Pdecline§! if no Envoy is assigned to it, or if its existence is exposed through covert §SOperations§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:155`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：谍报活动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

Tutorial: Espionage

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:156`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S谍报§!页面展示了我方£envoy£§S特使§!正如何在其他帝国内构建间谍网。你可以在此处理§S情报簿§!，或是查看及发起§S行动§!。\n\n我方可以在有§S特使§!经营间谍网的帝国内执行情报行动。各类行动都需要间谍网达到特定等级方能发起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

The §SEspionage§! tab shows how our £envoy£ §SEnvoys§! are building Spy Networks within other Empires. You can access the §SIntel ledger§! here, as well as view and launch §SOperations§!.\n\nOperations may be carried out in Empires where we have an §SEnvoy§! managing a Spy Network. Each type of Operation requires the Spy Network to have reached a certain level before it may be launched.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:157`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_OPERATIONS_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_OPERATIONS_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：谍报行动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Tutorial: Espionage Operations

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:158`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_OPERATIONS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_OPERATIONS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

既然我方间谍网等级已经足够，我们可以发起针对此帝国的§S行动§!。这些行动分为若干类别，各个类别的行动会有不同的结果——有些行动比别的行动更加危险。\n\n行动类别包括§S$op_cat_subterfuge$§!、§S$op_cat_manipulation$§!、§S$op_cat_sabotage$§!和§S$op_cat_provocation$§!。蓄意破坏和煽风点火类行动的要求更加苛刻，如果被抓住的话§S外交惩罚§!也更为严重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Now that our Spy Network has reached a sufficient level, we may launch §SOperations§! against this Empire. These are arranged in categories, with different types of Operation leading to different outcomes - some more perilous than others.\n\nThe Operation categories comprise §S$op_cat_subterfuge$§!, §S$op_cat_manipulation$§!, §S$op_cat_sabotage$§! and §S$op_cat_provocation$§!. Sabotage and Provocation-type Operations tend to be more demanding, and carry more serious §Sdiplomatic penalties§! if we are caught.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:159`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_ASSIGN_ASSET_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_ASSIGN_ASSET_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：指派线人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Tutorial: Assigning Assets

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:160`

## TUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_ASSIGN_ASSET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ESPIONAGE_VIEW_ASSIGN_ASSET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S线人§!是有价值的特工和物件，能为我方情报活动提供不同效果。线人在间谍网内发挥作用，也可以指派到行动当中，以发掘新的机会或是帮助我们摆脱困境。\n\n我们可以通过事件或者执行§S招募线人§!行动来得到线人。如果某个间谍网不复存在，其线人也会一并丢失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

§SAssets§! are valuable agents and artifacts which provide different effects for our Espionage activities. They work within the Spy Network and may be assigned to Operations, where they can open up new opportunities or else help us out of trouble.\n\nWe may receive Assets through events, or by performing the §SAcquire Asset§! Operation. If a Spy Network ceases to exist, its Assets are also lost.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:161`

## TUTORIAL_MEGASTRUCTURE_CONSTRUCTION_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_MEGASTRUCTURE_CONSTRUCTION_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：巨型结构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Tutorial: Megastructures

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:162`

## TUTORIAL_MEGASTRUCTURE_CONSTRUCTION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_MEGASTRUCTURE_CONSTRUCTION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在此列表中，我们可以选择一种£megastructure£§S巨型结构§!并在此£system£§S恒星系§!内[GetBuildGrow]。某些巨型结构必须先研发相应的§S科技§!才可建造。\n\n我的宇宙雄心子例程已经完全激活了；我建议你好好把握这些机会，为星系印上属于你的恒久烙印。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

From this list, we can choose a £megastructure£ §SMegastructure§! to [GetBuildGrow] inside this £system£ §SSolar System§!. Some projects only become available after we have discovered and researched a relevant §STechnology§!.\n\nMy Galactic Ambition subroutines have now been fully activated; I recommend you seize these opportunities to make a lasting impression upon the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:163`

## TUTORIAL_ORBITAL_RING_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ORBITAL_RING_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星环基地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Tutorial: Orbital Ring

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:164`

## TUTORIAL_ORBITAL_RING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ORBITAL_RING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S星环基地§!是一种能够环抱整颗£planet£§S行星§!的£megastructure£§S巨型结构§!。它能搭建起一条支持空地间高效运转的通道，为诸如船坞、防御结构、资源仓库之类的的建筑提供空间。\n\n在星环基地界面我们可以修建或者移除£building£§S建筑§!或是§S模块§!，也可以升级星环基地至更高等级。升级基地可以增加可用的槽位数量，不过等级上限受到殖民地基础设施的限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

§SOrbital Rings§! are vast £megastructure£ §SMegastructures§! which are built to encircle an entire £planet£ §SPlanet§!. They make for an efficient bridge between space and the planet below, providing room for facilities like Shipyards, defensive structures, and resource hubs.\n\nThe Orbital Ring view is where we can add or remove £building£ §SBuildings§! and §SModules§!, as well as upgrade the Orbital Ring to higher tiers. Upgrading tiers increases the number of slots we have available to build in. Tiers are limited by colony infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:165`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_CONFIGURABLE_TERMS_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_CONFIGURABLE_TERMS_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：条款配置界面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Tutorial: Configurable Terms

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:166`

## TUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_CONFIGURABLE_TERMS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DIPLOMACY_CONFIGURABLE_TERMS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

该界面允许你为协议选择§S预设模板§!，并在其基础上增减个性化条款。每种预设模板都为附属国和宗主国提供了一些特定的责任和义务。\n\n你可以对§S条款§!进行调整，达到改变其他国家§S忠诚度§!的效果，以使得协议对双方而言都容易接受。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

This screen allows us to choose a §SPreset§! for our Agreement, and to configure its individual Terms. Each preset provides a tailored set of responsibilities and obligations for both Subject and Overlord.\n\nWe can make changes in the list of §STerms§! which will adjust §SLoyalty§! to the other empire's liking, making the Agreement more appealing to both parties.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:167`

## TUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：协议总览

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Tutorial: Agreements Overview

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:168`

## TUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S协议§!界面向我们展示附属国与宗主国的各种协议。我们可以利用该界面追踪§S忠诚度§!的变化，管理§S地产§!，开展§S贸易§!，或是修订现有协议的§S条款§!。如果一项协议不再符合我国利益，我们也可以完全废除该项协议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

The §SAgreements§! screen provides an overview of our Subject and Overlord Agreements. We can use this screen to keep track of §SLoyalty§!, manage §SHoldings§! and §STrade§!, and renegotiate the §STerms§! of existing Agreements. We may also end an Agreement entirely, if it no longer suits our purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:169`

## TUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_HAS_OVERLORD_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_HAS_OVERLORD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$TUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_DESC$\n\n附属国可以选择成为§S专精国家§!。专精能带来巨幅的增益，例如资源生产或军事实力，但国家也会同时在其他方面承受惩罚。\n\n专精属性同时也能提供§S忠诚度§!增长。附属国如果维持忠诚度超过一定数值，其也将持续获得经验，并用于提升选择的专精类型等级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

$TUTORIAL_AGREEMENTS_DESC$\n\nSubject empires may choose to become §SSpecialist Empires§!. Specialization brings significant benefits to matters like resource production or military capability, however the empire will find itself at a disadvantage in other areas.\n\nSpecialist Empires also provide a boost to §SLoyalty§!. So long as they maintain a stable amount of Loyalty, Specialist Empires will stand to earn XP and thus progress towards higher tiers for their chosen specialty.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:173`

## TUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：地产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

Tutorial: Holdings

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:174`

## TUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_SHARED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_SHARED&lang=en)

### 中文

§S地产§!界面能够提供这颗£planet£§S行星§!上所有地产的总览。这允许国家直接相互在首都与£colony£§S殖民地§!上修建不同类型的£building£§S建筑§!。该行为能对诸如外交，情报或者资源生产等方面产生影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

The §SHoldings§! screen provides an overview of all the Holdings on this £planet£ §SPlanet§!. This allows empires to place distinct types of £building£ §SBuildings§! on each other's capitals and £colony£ §SColonies§!. These may affect matters such as Diplomacy, Intel, and resource production.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:175`

## TUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_MEGA_CORP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_MEGA_CORP&lang=en)

### 中文

只要先进行一些外交准备，寰宇企业能够在其他帝国星球上开设§S分部§!。众所周知，大型企业总是希望他们的顾客得到最好的服务，尽管这已经是第23周期的老话了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

Megacorporations may open §SBranch Offices§! on other empires' worlds, provided some form of Diplomatic arrangement is in place. As we know, large corporations wish only the best for their customers - events of the 23rd cycle notwithstanding!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:176`

## TUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_OVERLORD

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_DESC_OVERLORD&lang=en)

### 中文

§S宗主国§!可以利用地产来加深对于§S附属国§!的控制。许多地产都能对忠诚度产生影响，其中有些只能在对应专精的国家修建。你也可以在此界面对地产进行各种升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

§SOverlords§! may use Holdings to further extend their reach into a §SSubject§!'s domain. Many such Holdings affect Loyalty, and some are uniquely tailored to Specialist Empires. Any upgrades may also be facilitated via this screen.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:177`

$TUTORIAL_HOLDINGS_TITLE$

### English

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_COLONY_GOV_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_COLONY_GOV_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

要保障£colony£§S殖民地§!的发展，我们需要招募一名殖民地§S总督§!监督运作。\n\n大多数星球能够在没有§S总督§!的情况下正常运转，并获得基于整体的增益。但对于那些比较幸运的£planet£§S行星§!，§S总督§!能够带来极大的加强！\n\n总督会对其所管辖的星球产生各种不同的影响，其中大部分影响是正面的。总督的影响取决于其§S特质§!与§S总督§!能力等级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

To help our new £colony£ §SColony§! along, we should consider the recruitment of a §SGovernor§! to oversee operations there.\n\nMost of our worlds will get by swimmingly without the luxury of a §SGovernor§!, and benefit instead from a bonus depending on our government type. But to those £planet£ §SPlanets§! fortunate enough, a §SGovernor§! can be a great boon!\n\nGovernors can have a number of different effects on the planet they govern, most of them positive. These effects depend on the §STraits§! and skill level of the §SGovernor§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:187`

## TUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S选举§!界面中我们可以选定帝国新一任的统治者。\n\n[TutorialElectionViewTypeSpecific]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

The §SElection§! screen is where we decide on a new ruler for our empire.\n\n[TutorialElectionViewTypeSpecific]

## TUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DEMOCRACY_NO

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DEMOCRACY_NO&lang=en)

### 中文

候选人将从你现有的£leader£ §S领袖§!中产生，如果你拥有足够的资源，可以使选举胜利的天平向你的意中之人倾斜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Valid candidates are picked from your current £leader£ §SLeaders§!, and if you have resources to spare the choice of ruler is yours.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:190`

## TUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DEMOCRACY_YES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ELECTION_VIEW_DEMOCRACY_YES&lang=en)

### 中文

候选人将从你现有的£leader£ §S领袖§!中产生，你可以提升他们的支持度，当然啦，肯定是以最民主的方式提升的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Valid candidates are picked from your current £leader£ §SLeaders§! and they can be promoted to increase their Support - in the most democratic way possible, of course.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:191`
