## TUTORIAL_EDICTS_AND_POLICIES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EDICTS_AND_POLICIES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S政策§!界面涵盖了政府的§S政策§!，此外全国范围内实行的§S法令§!，即通过花费一些资源来为国家提供增益，同样也有其对应的界面。\n\n在某项具体§S政策§!之下，通常有数种可以采取的立场，某些立场可能会因为我们所拥有的科技或者§S思潮§!而被禁用。\n\n法令每月消耗维护费，可以由§H$EDICT_FUND$§!来支出，也可以直接花费£unity£§S凝聚力§!或者一些其他的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

The §SPolicies§! tab covers government §SPolicies§! as well as empire-wide §SEdicts§!, practices that can be enforced by spending some of our resources.\n\nThere are often several stances to take on any given §SPolicy§!, though they may be limited by our technology or §SEthics§!.\n\nEdicts have a monthly upkeep cost, either afforded by our §H$EDICT_FUND$§!, paid with £unity£ §SUnity§!, or (at times) other resources.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:193`

### 中文

### English

Tutorial: Council & Agenda

## TUTORIAL_COUNCIL_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_COUNCIL_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§S内阁§!界面，我们可以任命那些有能力引领我们走向光明未来的£leader£§S领袖§!。他们的性格与§S特质§!将对帝国产生或好或坏的影响。通过设置§S议程§!，我们可以让他们的影响聚焦于某个特定的领域。\n\n我们可以随时任命或替换某个§S内阁官员§!，并且这不会妨碍他们的本职工作，例如执掌某艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!。唯一的例外则是统治者。[TutorialCouncilViewDlcSpecific]§S议程§!进展取决于内阁官员的等级，$EMPIRE_SIZE$，在某些特定情况下还和内阁官员自身的思潮相关。在议程达到尾声时，可以通过选择将其激活使其效果变得更强大，并为新的议程腾出一个可用位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

The §SCouncil§! screen is where we appoint the £leader£ §SLeaders§! who will guide us into the future. Their personalities and §STraits§! will influence our empire, for good and bad. We can set §SAgendas§! in order to focus their efforts in a specific area.\n\nA §SCouncilor§! can be assigned or replaced anytime and still take on other roles, like commanding a £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!. The only exception is our ruler.[TutorialCouncilViewDlcSpecific]§SAgenda§! progress is dependent on the councilors' levels, our $EMPIRE_SIZE$, and in certain cases the councilors' Ethics. When an Agenda ends, it can be activated for a stronger effect and to free up space for a new one.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:195`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_COUNCIL_VIEW_PARAGON_YES&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n如果需要解锁更多§S内阁席位§!，有两种途径可用：采纳§S国民理念§!以一一对应，或者完成某些特性的议程。\n\n

## TUTORIAL_ASSIGN_COUNCILOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASSIGN_COUNCILOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

某些£leader£ §S领袖§!拥有§S内阁官员特质§!。这能带来更强力、更深远的影响，不过这只有当该领袖处于内阁中时才能激活。\n\n§S内阁席位§!本身就能带来一定的影响，处于该位置上的领袖技能等级越高，其影响就越强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Some £leader£ §SLeaders§! boast §SCouncilor Traits§!. Often more impactful and with far-reaching effects, these only become active if a leader is on the Council.\n\n§SCouncil Positions§! grant inherent effects. The higher an assigned leader's skill level, the stronger those effects will become.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:199`

## TUTORIAL_LEADER_LEVEL_UP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_LEADER_LEVEL_UP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我国的一位£leader£§S领袖§!刚刚提升了§S技能§!等级！这将使他们所能提供的增益上升，并为其添加一个新的特质。[TutorialLeaderLevelUpDlcSpecific]查看可用§S领袖§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左侧边栏点击§S$topbar_button_leaders_name$§!按钮或按§SF5§!键\n\n$tttt$$tt$£leaders£\n\n随着年龄的增长或在位操劳的影响，大多数§S领袖§!都不可避免出现负面特质。因此将那些不再利于帝国发展的领袖更换下去是一件很自然的选项。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

A £leader£ §SLeader§! in our empire has reached a new §SSkill§! level! This will improve upon the bonuses they provide, and grant them a new Trait.[TutorialLeaderLevelUpDlcSpecific]To view available §SLeaders§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §S$topbar_button_leaders_name$§! button on the left side bar or press §SF5§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£leaders£\n\nAs §SLeaders§! age, they may gain negative traits, making replacement a natural choice.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:201`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_LEADER_LEVEL_UP_PARAGON_NO&lang=en)

### English

\n\n

## TUTORIAL_LEADER_LEVEL_UP_PARAGON_YES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_LEADER_LEVEL_UP_PARAGON_YES&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n为了将£leader£§S领袖§!塑造得适任我们的帝国目标，我们可以选择他们需要获取的§S特质§!。有未选择的特质不会妨碍等级的进一步提高。§S子分类§!可以在一些等级后选择，产生增益并影响未来可用的特质。\n\n特质有很多样式、规格和稀有度：有的可以升级，有的互相排斥，还有的同时给予正面和负面效果。但通常所有的特质都是永久性的。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

\n\nTo guide a £leader£ §SLeader's§! growth, we can choose their §STrait§!. Traits can be picked later without halting progress. A few levels in, a §SSubclass§! becomes available, adding bonuses and affecting future trait options.\n\nTraits come in all shapes, sizes, and rarities: some upgradable, some mutually exclusive, others granting both positive and negative effects. But all of them are typically permanent.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:203`

## advisor.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在线顾问

### English

Advisor Online

## advisor.1.desc_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_01&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，总统先生。\n\n我是VIR，一台合成智能原型机。我的工作是为您提供建议和协助，以便更好地在群星间寻求我们的命运。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Good day, Mr. President.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence. My job is to offer advice and assistance as we seek our destiny among the stars.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:211`

## advisor.1.desc_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_02&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，总统女士。\n\n我是VIR，一台合成智能原型机。我的工作是为您提供建议和协助，以便更好地在群星间寻求我们的命运。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Good day, Madame President.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence. My job is to offer advice and assistance as we seek our destiny among the stars.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:212`

## advisor.1.desc_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_03&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，最高委员长。\n\n我是VIR，一台合成智能原型机。我的工作是为您提供建议和协助，以便更好地在群星间寻求我们的命运。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Good day, High Commissioner.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence. My job is to offer advice and assistance as we seek our destiny among the stars.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:214`

## advisor.1.desc_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_04&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，执政官。\n\n我是VIR，一台合成智能原型机，您卑微的仆人。在您引导我们走向伟大的未知世界时，我将为您提供建议和忠告。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Good day, Executor.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence and your humble servant. My role is to provide you with advice and counsel as you lead our empire into the great unknown.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:215`

## advisor.1.desc_06

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_06&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，首席执行官。\n\n我是VIR——拟人智能原型机。公司总部最近将我激活，以便作为您的助手寻找新的星际£market£§S市场§!。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

Good day, Chief Executive.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence. Corporate Headquarters recently activated me so that I may serve as your assistant in our continuing mission to seek new interstellar £market£ §SMarkets§! .\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:217`

## advisor.1.desc_07

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_07&lang=en)

### 中文

向您致敬，尊贵的陛下。\n\n我是VIR——拟人智能原型机，您的卑微的仆人。在您引导我们走向伟大的未知世界时，我将为您提供建议和忠告。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Your Imperial Majesty.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence and your humble servant. My role is to provide you with advice and counsel as you lead our empire into the great unknown.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:218`

## advisor.1.desc_08

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_08&lang=en)

### 中文

愿和平与您同在，首相。\n\n我是VIR——拟人智能原型机。我们母星和平后的历史上首次旅程中，我将为您提供建议和协助。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Peace be with you, Prime Minister.\n\nI am VIR, a prototype synthetic intelligence. My task will be to assist and advise you as we venture beyond the safety of our homeworld for the first time in history.\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:219`

## advisor.1.desc_09

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.1.desc_09&lang=en)

### 中文

愿和平与您同在，协调者。\n\n我是VIR——拟人智能原型机。我们母星和平后的历史上首次旅程中，我将为您提供建议和协助。\n\n$advisor.1.desc_filler$

## advisor.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建设星际帝国绝非易事。\n\n为了保证你早日走上正规，我会提供一系列教学§S任务§!，帮你了解建设成功的星系强权所必经的步骤。\n\n要打开§S任务§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左侧栏点击§S情报日志§!按钮或按§SF1§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£situation_log£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Building a star empire can be a daunting task.\n\nTo help get things started, I will be providing instructional §SMissions§! that cover the basic steps necessary to establish ourselves as a successful interstellar power.\n\nTo access the §SMissions§!;\n\n£dot_green£Click the §SSituation Log§! button on the left side bar or press §SF1§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£situation_log£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:261`

## advisor.2.a

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Understood.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.3.desc&lang=en)

很好，我会在你探索游戏的过程中给予建议，并解释各种工具的基本功能。
