## advisor.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：异常现象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

Tutorial: Anomalies

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:265`

## advisor.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一个§S异常现象§!！\n\n有时候，我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!会在探索宇宙的过程中发现一些奇怪的事情。\n\n如果£scientist£§S科学官§!的等级足够的话，他们就能研究§S异常现象§!。\n\n被发现但是尚未研究的§S异常现象§!会被列在§S情报日志§!的§S异常现象§!选项卡里。在殖民或是进行其它建设之前，£planet£§S行星§!上的异常现象必须先被研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

We have found an §SAnomaly§!!\n\nFrom time to time, our £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! will make these discoveries while surveying alien worlds.\n\n§SAnomalies§! can be researched by a £scientist£ §SScientist§!, if their skills are up to the task!\n\nUnresearched §SAnomalies§! are listed under the §SAnomalies§! tab in the §SSituation Log§!. Some must be investigated before the £planet£ §SPlanet§! they were found on can be colonized or utilized.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:266`

## advisor.15.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.15.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：殖民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

Tutorial: Colonization

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:267`

## advisor.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!发现了十分适合作为我们第一个£colony£§S殖民地§!的星球。它和我们母星的§S行星类型§!相同，所以我们的殖民者应该可以住得很舒适。\n\n要殖民这个星球的话，要先拓展我们的边界。\n\n选中£construction_ship£§S工程船§!；\n\n£dot_green£右键点击一个调查完毕且含有潜在殖民地的£system£§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S[GetBuildGrowCap]恒星基地§!\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£colonizable£\n\n当宣称拥有了该§S恒星系§!后，我们就可以开始殖民流程了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

One of our £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! has just surveyed a world that would make an excellent candidate for our first £colony£ §SColony§!. It is of the same §Splanet class§! as our homeworld, so our colonists should be quite comfortable there.\n\nBefore colonizing this world, we must extend our borders.\n\nWith the £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! selected;\n\n£dot_green£ Right-click a surveyed £system£ §SSystem§! containing a potential colony\n£dot_green£ Select §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Starbase§!\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£colonizable£\n\nOnce the §SSystem§! is claimed, we can begin the colonization process.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:268`

## advisor.17.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.17.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：首个殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Tutorial: First Colony

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:269`

## advisor.17.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.17.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

祝贺你殖民了一颗新星球！殖民者正在建造他们的第一个殖民地，想要让他们自给自足，则还需要一段时间。\n\n£colony£§S§!殖民地在第一个定居点建设完成完成前会不断消耗我们的资源，所以不要一次性对过多£planet£§S行星§!进行殖民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Congratulations on having colonized a new world! Our colonists are now in the process of building their first settlement, but it will take time for them to become self-sufficient.\n\nThe £colony£ §SColony§! will be a drain on our resources until the first settlement has been completed, so take care not to colonize too many £planet£ §SPlanets§! at once.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:270`

## advisor.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：负收入

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

Tutorial: Negative Income

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:271`

## advisor.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正以可怕的速度损失£energy£能量币，上个月的预算报表情况十分糟糕。\n\n维护费是财政赤字的常见原因。如果不幸言中，则应该解散一部分飞船，部队或者拆除太空港。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

We are losing £energy£ Energy Credits at an alarming rate. Last month's budget report makes for some grim reading.\n\nSpiraling upkeep costs are a common cause of budget deficits. If this is the case, we may want to consider disbanding some of our ships, armies or stations.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:272`

## tutorial.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星系地图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Tutorial: Galaxy Map

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:283`

## tutorial.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H星系地图§!可以提供对群星的大局观。可以用它来导航、扩张、指挥舰队以及标注边境。\n\n打开§S星系地图§!；\n\n£dot_green£在屏幕下方点击§H星系§!图标或按§HM§!键\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£galaxy_view£\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

The §HGalaxy Map§! offers a strategic view of the stars. Use it to navigate, expand, command fleets, and monitor borders.\n\nTo open the §SGalaxy Map§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §HGalaxy§! icon at the bottom of the screen or press §HM§!\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£galaxy_view£\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:284`

## tutorial.2.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星系调查完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Tutorial: System Surveyed

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:285`

## tutorial.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好！\n\n对外£system£§S恒星系§!的第一次调查圆满完成了。\n\n我们母星上区区一代人之前的天文学家们梦寐以求的行星数据现在已尽在掌握。\n\n继续调查；\n\n£dot_green£选择一艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!\n£dot_green£右键点击邻近§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S调查恒星系§!\n\n§S恒星系§!必须完成调查后才能宣称拥有。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Well done!\n\nThe first survey of a £system£ §SStar System§! beyond our own has been completed.\n\nWe now have access to planetary data that the astronomers on our homeworld could only dream of a mere generation ago.\n\nTo continue surveying;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!\n£dot_green£ Right-click a neighboring §SSystem§!\n£dot_green£ Select §SSurvey System§!\n\nA §SSystem§! must be surveyed before it can be claimed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:286`

## tutorial.3.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.3.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：科技

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Tutorial: Technologies

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:287`

## tutorial.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究新科技对我们成功至关重要。我这么说不仅是因为我自己就是科技的产物，更是因为我是你的可靠顾问，还有可能是你唯一真正的朋友。\n\n我们应该最先研究§S新世界协议§!，这样就可以建造£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，在其他£planet£§S行星§!上立足。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Researching new technologies will be critical to our success. I tell you this not merely as a product of unfettered technological progress, but as your trusted advisor and, I suspect, your only true friend.\n\nOne important technology that we should research as soon as possible is §SNew Worlds Protocol§!. It will give us access to £colony_ship£ §SColony Ships§! which will allow us to establish footholds on other £planet£ §SPlanets§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:288`

## tutorial.4.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.4.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Tutorial: Survey

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:289`

## tutorial.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欲拓其土，必先丈量之。而我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!正是为此事而生。\n\n你可以用如下图标识别出§S科研船§!：\n\n$tttt$$tt$£science_ship_map£\n\n\n选择舰船的方式有两种；在§S星系地图§!或§S总览§!中点选。\n\n£dot_green£选择一艘§S科研船§!\n£dot_green£右键点击邻近£system£§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S调查恒星系§!\n\n§S恒星系§!必须先完成调查之后才能宣称占领。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Before we can expand we must explore, and our £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! are the vessel of choice for such a task.\n\nYou can identify a §SScience Ships§! by the following symbol:\n\n$tttt$$tt$£science_ship_map£\n\n\nThere are two ways of selecting a ship; From the §SGalaxy Map§! or the §SOutliner§!.\n\n£dot_green£ Select a §SScience Ship§!\n£dot_green£ Right-click a nearby £system£ §SSystem§!\n£dot_green£ Select §SSurvey System§!\n\nA §SSystem§! must be surveyed before it can be claimed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:290`

## tutorial.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

取消暂停

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

Unpausing

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:291`

## tutorial.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在我们已经可以向我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!下达出发的命令了。我们会需要取消§S星系地图§!模拟的暂停才能让一切继续。\n\n§S取消暂停§!；\n\n£dot_green£可以按§S空格键§!或是顶部栏的§S取消暂停§!按钮：\n\n$tttt$$t$£pause_button£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Now that we have given a command to our £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! it's time to set off. We will need to unpause the §SGalaxy Map§! simulation before anything can happen.\n\nTo §Sunpause§! the game;\n\n£dot_green£ Press the §SSpace Bar§! or select the §SUnpause§! icon in the top bar\n\n$tttt$$t$£pause_button£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:292`

## tutorial.6.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.6.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：科技研究完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

Tutorial: Technology Researched

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:293`

## tutorial.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好！刚刚研究完成的科技对我们作用很大。别忘了为£scientist£§S科学官§!们安排新的研究项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

Excellent work! The technology we just finished researching will certainly benefit us. Don't forget to give our £scientist£ §SScientists§! new research assignments.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:294`

## tutorial.7.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.7.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：工程船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Tutorial: Construction Ship

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:295`

## tutorial.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们母星所能提供的资源也就只能支持我们走到这一步。我们的产业需要扩张到外星，这就需要有一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!。\n\n如果我们收集到了足够的£alloys£§S合金§!，就赶紧[GetBuildGrow]一艘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

The resources generated by our homeworld will only take us so far. Our industry needs to expand off-world, and that requires a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!.\n\nWe should [GetBuildGrow] one as soon as we have amassed enough £alloys£ §SAlloys§! to do so.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:296`

## tutorial.8.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.8.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：工程船建造完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Tutorial: Construction Ship Built

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:297`

## tutorial.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好！£construction_ship£§S工程船§!可以运行了，上面的船员可都是帝国最棒的工程师。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Good job! Our £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! is now up and running, and its hardy crew includes some of the finest engineers available to our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:298`

## tutorial.9.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.9.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：开采站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

Tutorial: Mining Station

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:299`

## tutorial.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

母星上生产的资源只够我们走到这一步，好在我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!在另外一颗星球上发现了一处资源矿藏！\n\n是时候派£construction_ship£§S工程船§![GetBuildGrow]一座£mining_station£§S开采站§!了。\n\n§S开采站§!可以在帝国境内建造，它能自动采集所环绕天体上的£minerals£§S矿物§!、£energy£§S能量币§!以及£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!。\n\n选择§S工程船§!；\n\n£dot_green£右键点击一个境内£system£§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S[GetBuildGrowCap]开采站§!\n\n§S开采站§!需要花费§S能量币§!作为维护费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

The resources generated by our homeworld will only take us so far. Luckily our £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! has just found a resource deposit on another world!\n\nThe time has come to use our £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! to [GetBuildGrow] a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§!.\n\n§SMining Stations§! can be built within our borders and automatically collect £minerals£ §SMinerals§!, £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§!, and £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§!.\n\nWith the §SConstruction Ship§! selected;\n\n£dot_green£ Right-click a claimed £system£ §SSystem§!\n£dot_green£ Select §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Mining Stations§!\n\n§SMining Stations§! have an §SEnergy Credit§! upkeep cost.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:300`

## tutorial.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：开采站建收益

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Tutorial: Mining Station Income

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:301`

## tutorial.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好，新造好的£mining_station£§S开采站§!已经开始运行，采集将用于进一步处理的矿物了。\n\n各种空间站的收入会直接加入帝国的总收入中，并显示在顶部栏里。将鼠标悬停在不同的资源图标上可以查看收支明细。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Very good. The new £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! is operational, and it has already begun gathering resources for processing.\n\nIncome from various stations is added to the empire's total income, which is displayed on the top bar. Hovering over different resources reveals a breakdown of income sources.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:302`

## tutorial.11.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.11.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：研究站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Tutorial: Research Station

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:303`

## tutorial.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

调查过的星球有些会具有特殊性质，是非常好的研究项目。这些£physics£§S物理学§!，£society£§S社会学§!和£engineering£§S工程学§!资源可以用£science_ship£§S研究站§!采集。我的程序强烈建议我敦促你建造一个研究站。\n\n记住§S研究站§!只能在帝国境内建造，它们需要£energy£ §S能量币§!来维持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Surveyed worlds sometimes possess unique properties that make them valuable objects of research. These £physics£ §SPhysics§!, £society£ §SSociety§! or £engineering£ §SEngineering§! resources can be collected by a £science_ship£ §SResearch Station§!. My programming strongly compels me to compel you to build one.\n\nKeep in mind that §SResearch Stations§! can only be built within our borders, and have a upkeep cost in £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:304`

## tutorial.12.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.12.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：研究站建造完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Tutorial: Research Station Built

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:305`

## tutorial.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的£science_ship£§S研究站§!已经上线，科学研究人员开始工作。这会解开什么谜团，揭示什么神秘事情呢？！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Our new £science_ship£ §SResearch Station§! is online, and its science staff have begun their work. What secrets will they uncover, what forbidden mysteries of Creation?!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:306`

## tutorial.13.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.13.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Tutorial: Planets

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:307`

## tutorial.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宜居£planet£§S星球§!划分了若干£district£§S区划§!，§S特区§!以及£building£§S建筑§!。\n\n£dot_green£§S区划§!可以由多个§S特区§!构成\n£dot_green£§S特区§!决定产生的£job£§S岗位§!类型\n£dot_green£§S建筑§!对§S特区§!提供增益\n\n随着特区的升级将解锁越来越多的§S建筑§!槽。\n\n有两种方式查看行星：\n从§S星系地图§!或是§S总览§!\n\n£dot_green£选择一颗§S行星§!\n£dot_green£点击各个选项卡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Habitable £planet£ §SPlanets§! are divided into £district£ §SDistricts§!, §SZones§!, and £building£ §SBuildings§!.\n\n£dot_green£§SDistricts§! can consist of multiple §SZones§!\n£dot_green£§SZones§! determine which £job£ §SJobs§! are created\n£dot_green£§SBuildings§! grant bonuses to the §SZones§!\n\nMore §SBuilding§! slots are unlocked as the zones are upgraded.\n\nThere are two ways of viewing a planet;\nFrom the §SGalaxy Map§! or the §SOutliner§!.\n\n£dot_green£ Select a §SPlanet§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the different tabs

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:308`

## tutorial.14.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.14.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：建筑优先安排

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Tutorial: Prioritize Building

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:309`

## tutorial.14.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.14.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好，新的§S殖民地建筑§!已经建好，应该可以开始生产资源了。\n\n随着£pops£§S人口§!的增长，他们会被自动指派到£building£§S建筑§!所产生的£job£§S岗位§!上，当然也可能会在不同岗位间切换。\n\n优先一座§S建筑§!；\n\n£dot_green£在§S总览§!选择£planet£§S行星§!\n£dot_green£点击§S经济§!选项卡\n£dot_green£点击一座§S建筑§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

Excellent. Construction has been finished on our new §SColony Building§!, and it should now begin producing resources.\n\n£pops£ §SPops§! will automatically be assigned to any £job£ §SJobs§! created by £building£ §SBuildings§! as the population grows, but can also be encouraged to switch jobs.\n\nTo prioritize a §SBuilding§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select the £planet£ §SPlanet§! in the §SOutliner§!\n£dot_green£ Click the §SEconomy§! tab\n£dot_green£ Click on a §SBuilding§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:310`

## tutorial.15.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.15.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：行星障碍

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

Tutorial: Planetary Blockers

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:311`

## tutorial.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宜居£planet£§S星球§!上有时会有£blocker£§S障碍§!，妨碍开发利用。清理§S障碍特征§!需要花费时间和£energy£§S能量币§!，但可能会解锁被其暂时阻碍的§S行星特征§!，还可以腾出新的建设空间。\n\n清理§S障碍§!；\n\n£dot_green£在§S总览§!中选择一颗行星\n£dot_green£点击§S管理§!选项卡\n£dot_green£在障碍上点击§S清理障碍§!按钮\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£clear_blocker£\n\n\n除了资源以外，一些§S障碍§!还需要额外的§S科技§!来移除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Habitable £planet£ §SPlanets§! sometimes have £blocker£ §SBlockers§!, preventing their full exploitation. Clearing a §SBlocker§! takes time and costs £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§!, but can unlock §SPlanetary Features§! currently blocked as well as free up additional construction space.\n\nTo clear a §SBlocker§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a planet in the §SOutliner§!\n£dot_green£ Click the §SManagement§! tab\n£dot_green£ Click the §SClear Blocker§! button on the blocker itself\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£clear_blocker£\n\n\nIn addition to resources, some §SBlockers§! require specific §STechnologies§! to remove.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:312`

## tutorial.16.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.16.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：障碍消除

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

Tutorial: Blocker Cleared

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:313`

## tutorial.16.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.16.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真不错！\n\n£blocker£§S障碍§!成功清除了。\n\n现在可以将鼠标悬停在任何§S障碍§!上，看看清理后都会解锁什么§S行星特征§!。\n\n只要收集到足够的资源就应该把剩余的§S障碍§!也清理掉，让这颗£planet£§S行星§!得以发挥全部潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

Well done!\n\nThe £blocker£ §SBlocker§! has been successfully removed.\n\nYou can hover over any remaining §SBlockers§! to see which §SPlanetary Feature§! will be unlocked once they are cleared.\n\nAny remaining §SBlockers§! should be cleared once enough resources have been gathered, to allow this £planet£ §SPlanet§! to reach its full potential.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:314`

## tutorial.17.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.17.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：调查星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

Tutorial: Survey Systems

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:315`

## tutorial.17.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.17.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该继续进行探索。我推荐派一艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!去调查周围的£system£§S星系§!。让我们勇敢地航向前人所未至的领域吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

We should continue our exploration efforts. I recommend sending a £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! to survey neighboring £system£ §SStar Systems§!. Let us boldly go where no synthetic intelligence has gone before!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:316`

## tutorial.19.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.19.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星系调查完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Tutorial: Systems Surveyed

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:317`

## tutorial.19.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.19.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好！邻近我们£system£§S母星系§!的的星系已经都调查完毕了。接下来就该派£construction_ship£§S工程船§!去[GetBuildingGrowing]£mining_station£§S开采站§!和£science_ship£§S研究站§!来获得我们发现的那些资源了。\n\n你可以用以下符号辨认出哪艘是§S工程船§!：\n\n$tttt$$tt$£construction_ship_map£

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

Great work! The stars neighboring our £system£ §SHome System§! have been fully explored. We should use our £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! to collect any resources that were found by [GetBuildingGrowing] £mining_station£ §SMining§! and £science_ship£ §SResearch Stations§!.\n\nYou can identify a §SConstruction Ship§! by the following symbol:\n\n$tttt$$tt$£construction_ship_map£

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:318`

## tutorial.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：[GetBuildGrowCap]科研船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

Tutorial: [GetBuildGrowCap] Science Ship

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:319`

## tutorial.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该考虑[GetBuildingGrowing]第二艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!了。这可以人我们加快对星系近邻的调查速度。\n\n[GetBuildGrow]§S舰船§!；\n\n£dot_green£选择一座§S恒星基地§!\n£dot_green£点击§S船坞§!选项卡\n£dot_green£点击§S科研船§!\n\n新造§S舰船§!在建造完毕后会出现在§S恒星基地§!旁边。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

We may want to consider [GetBuildingGrowing] a second £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!. This would double the speed at which we can survey our galactic neighborhood!\n\nTo [GetBuildGrow] a §SShip§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a §SStarbase§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the §SShipyard§! tab\n£dot_green£ Click on §SScience Ship§!\n\nThe newly constructed §SShip§! will appear next to the §SStarbase§! once completed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:320`

## tutorial.21.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.21.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：自动调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Tutorial: Survey Automation

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:321`

## tutorial.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

双倍£science_ship£§S科研船§!就会有双倍的乐趣！自从完成上个任务后，我还没那么兴奋过呢！\n\n操作多艘科研船探索费时费力。\n\n你可以通过选择§S科研船§!并在舰队交互界面点击§S科研船自动调查§!按钮自动化调查操作。\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£science_ship_automate_exploration£\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Double the £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! means double the fun! Oh, I haven't been this excited since we completed the last assignment!\n\nExploring with multiple ships can be time-consuming.\n\nYou can automate surveying by selecting the §SScience Ship§! and clicking the §SScience Ship Automation§! button in the fleet interface.\n\n\n$tttt$$tt$£science_ship_automate_exploration£\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:322`

## tutorial.22.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.22.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：招募科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Tutorial: Recruit Scientist

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:323`

## tutorial.22.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.22.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们建造了新的£science_ship£§S科研船§!，但是没有指派操控飞船的£scientist£§S科学官§!！我的程序要求我指出雇佣一名新的§S科学家§!的好处。\n\n选中§S科研船§!；\n\n£dot_green£在舰队界面点击空白头像框\n£dot_green£选择一位§S科学官；科学家§!\n\n如果无法承担雇佣一名新§S科学家§!的费用，也可以从其他部门调用一名，虽然不怎么理想就是了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

We have a brand new £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!, but no £scientist£ §SScientist§! has been assigned to command it. My programming insists that I should point out the benefits of adding an organic §SScientist§! component to the mix.\n\nWith the §SScience Ship§! selected;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the empty portrait in the fleet interface\n£dot_green£ Choose a §SScientist§!\n\nIf we cannot afford to recruit a new §SScientist§! we can always reassign one from another department, even though it is hardly ideal.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:324`

## tutorial.23.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.23.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：招募科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Tutorial: Scientist Recruited

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:325`

## tutorial.23.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.23.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好极了。£scientist£§S科学官§!已经安排到新的£science_ship£§S科研船§!上了，我们可以在闲时派遣他们出去探索新的£system£§S恒星系§!或者捅出些篓子了。\n\n如果想更换§S科学官§!，只需要在舰队界面再次点击头像框即可。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Good. The £scientist£ §SScientist§! has assumed command of our new £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!, and can now be sent off at our leisure to explore new £system£ §SSystems§! and/or meet some grisly fate.\n\nShould you wish to change the §SScientist§!, then simply select the portrait once more in the fleet interface.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:326`

## tutorial.24.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.24.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：[GetBuildGrowCap]殖民船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

Tutorial: [GetBuildGrowCap] Colony Ship

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:327`

## tutorial.24.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.24.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经准备好到下一个星球上延续和发展我们伟大的文明了，这时候就需要我们建造一艘£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!。\n\n[GetBuildGrow]§S舰船§!；\n\n£dot_green£选中一个§S恒星基地§!\n£dot_green£点击§S船坞§!选项卡\n£dot_green£点击§S殖民船舰船§!\n\n新建成的§S舰船§!在建造完毕后会出现在§S恒星基地§!旁边。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

We should now be ready to extend our great civilization to a second world. This will require the construction of a £colony_ship£ §SColony Ship§!.\n\nTo [GetBuildGrow] a §SShip§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a §SStarbase§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the §SShipyard§! tab\n£dot_green£ Click on §SColony Ship§!\n\nThe newly constructed §SShip§! will appear next to the §SStarbase§! once completed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:328`

## tutorial.25.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：殖民行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Tutorial: Colonize Planet

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:329`

## tutorial.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民者已经全部登上新建的£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，舰长表示随时可以出发。真是一个值得纪念的日子！\n\n建立£colony£§S殖民地§!有几种不同的方法。\n\n在§S星系地图§!上§S殖民§!£planet£§S行星§!；\n\n£dot_green£点击一颗可殖民§S行星§!\n£dot_green£点击§S殖民§!按钮\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£colonizable£\n\n殖民者们出发之后，要花费数年时间来建立殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

The colonists have all been transferred to the new £colony_ship£ §SColony Ship§!, and her captain reports all systems go. A momentous day, for sure!\n\nThere are different ways to establish a £colony£ §SColony§!.\n\nTo §SColonize§! a £planet£ §SPlanet§! on the §SGalaxy Map§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click a colonizable §SPlanet§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the §SColonize§! button\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£colonizable£\n\nOnce the colonists have disembarked, they will take a few years to fully establish the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:330`

## tutorial.26.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.26.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：建立殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

Tutorial: Establish Colony

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:331`

## tutorial.26.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.26.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们拥有了一艘£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，也发现了至少一处可以建造永久系外据点的星球。没理由犹豫了，快建立我们的第一个£colony£§S殖民地§!吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

We have a £colony_ship£ §SColony Ship§!, and we have found at least one suitable candidate for a permanent extrasolar outpost. I see no reason to delay further - let's establish our first £colony£ §SColony§!!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:332`

## tutorial.27.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.27.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：殖民地建立完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Tutorial: Colony Established

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:333`

## tutorial.27.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.27.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们的第一个系外£colony£§S殖民地§!！这样就算母星发生爆炸，也不用担心面临消灭的命运了！还不赖吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Our first extrasolar £colony£ §SColony§!! This means that if our homeworld were to spontaneously combust, your species would no longer be rendered extinct. That is a comforting thought, wouldn't you say?

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:334`

## tutorial.28.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.28.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：招募总督

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Tutorial: Recruit Governor

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:335`

## tutorial.28.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.28.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

要保障£colony£§S殖民地§!的发展，我们需要招募一名§S总督§!负责监督运作。\n\n总督会对£colony£§S殖民地§!产生各种不同的影响，其中大部分影响是正面的。总督的影响取决于其本身的个人特质及技能等级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

To help our new £colony£ §SColony§! along, we should consider the recruitment of a §SGovernor§! to oversee operations there.\n\nGovernors can have a number of different effects on the £colony£ §SColony§! they govern, most of them positive. These effects depend on the traits and skill level of the Governor.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:336`

## tutorial.29.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.29.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：总督招募完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Tutorial: Governor Recruited

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:337`

## tutorial.29.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.29.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的£colony£§S殖民地§!现在已经处于一名新§S总督§!管辖之下了。这会让我们更有效率！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

Our £colony£ §SColony§! is now administered by a new §SGovernor§!. This should increase its efficiency!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:338`

## tutorial.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：建造更多殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Tutorial: More Colonies

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:339`

## tutorial.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要我们有足够的资源，就可以[GetBuildGrow]更多的£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，扩张我们帝国的领域。我们应该努力再建立至少三个£colony£§S殖民地§!。加上我们的母星和现在这个殖民地，我们就能踏足五个星球了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

As soon as we have the resources, it would be a good idea to [GetBuildGrow] more £colony_ship£ §SColony Ships§! and expand the borders of our empire further. We should work towards establishing at least three more £colony£ §SColonies§! in addition to our homeworld and the one we just settled.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:340`

## tutorial.32.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.32.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：殖民地成立

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

Tutorial: Colonies Established

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:341`

## tutorial.32.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.32.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

干得漂亮！真是激动人心。我们高尚的文明已经散播到五个星球了！只要机会合适，我们需要继续探索，进行扩张。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

Brilliant work! Very inspiring. Our mighty civilization now extends to five worlds! We should keep expanding as opportunity allows.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:342`

## tutorial.33.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.33.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：资源生产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Tutorial: Resource Production

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:343`

## tutorial.33.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.33.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

稳定的£minerals£§S矿物§!、£alloys£§S合金§!与£energy£§S能量币§!供应是任何星际势力繁荣发展的关键。我们应当采取措施增加这些资源的产量。\n\n空间站和£building£§S建筑§!需要消耗合金或矿物来建造，还需要能量币来维护。§S舰船§!也是如此，只不过它们也需要合金来维护。这些资源由£mining_station£§S开采站§!或§S殖民地建筑§!生产。\n\n后期你会发现一些有趣的新资源，可用于神秘的新建造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

A healthy supply of £minerals£ §SMinerals§!, £alloys£ §SAlloys§! and £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§! is critical to the well-being of any interstellar power. We should take steps to increase our production of these resources.\n\nStations and £building£ §SBuildings§! cost Alloys or Minerals to build and Energy Credits to maintain. The same is true for §SShips§!, except that they have an Alloy upkeep cost as well. These resources are produced by £mining_station£ §SMining Stations§! or §SColony Buildings§!. \n\nLater you may discover new and interesting resources to spend on esoteric new production.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:344`

## tutorial.38.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.38.name&lang=en)

### 中文

资源生产成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Resource Production Obtained

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:345`

## tutorial.38.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.38.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

干得漂亮。这下我们有足够的资源去继续建设我们的宏伟帝国了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Excellent work. We now have a strong resource base to build our empire on.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:346`

## tutorial.39.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.39.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：舰队增编

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

Tutorial: Increase the Fleet

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:347`

## tutorial.39.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.39.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队有点弱不禁风。在宇宙中有各种各样的危险，需要我们用武力手段进行回应。要记住，我们的文明时刻处于危险之中！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

I can't help but notice that our navy is a bit on the weak side. We are certain to face many threats in the galaxy that will require some form of military response. Remember, the survival of our civilization is at stake!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:348`

## tutorial.43.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.43.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队规模扩大

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Navy Size Increased

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:349`

## tutorial.43.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.43.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了！我们的舰队小而精悍，应该能在突发的敌对遭遇中拥有上佳的表现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

Good! We now have a small but capable space fleet that should serve us well in the event of any unexpected hostile encounters.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:350`

## tutorial.44.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.44.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：建立星域

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Tutorial: Establish Sector

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:351`

## tutorial.44.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.44.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的帝国蓬勃发展，规模已经大到我们没法通过母星直接进行控制了。我们应该建立新的§S星域§!。\n\n星域是属于我们帝国的区域，能设为自主管理模式，居民可以在他们的权限范围内自行负责£planet£§S行星§!上的日常事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Our burgeoning empire has now reached a size where it is becoming difficult to administer directly from our homeworld. We should establish a new §SSector§!.\n\nSectors are areas within our empire that can be set to independently manage the day-to-day affairs of the £planet£ §SPlanets§! under their jurisdiction.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:352`

## tutorial.45.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.45.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：星域成立

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Tutorial: Sector Established

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:353`

## tutorial.45.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.45.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着帝国的发展，新的£colony£§S殖民地§!可以被划归进§S星域§!。星域可以在§S星球与星域§!界面进行自动管理，自动对£district£§S区划§!与£building£§S建筑§!进行建造与升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

As our empire grows, new £colony£ §SColonies§! can be organized into §SSectors§!. Sectors can be automated from the §SPlanets and Sectors§! screen to automatically build and upgrade £district£ §SDistricts§! and £building£ §SBuildings§! using assigned pools of resources.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:354`

## tutorial.46.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.46.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：总督

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Tutorial: Sector Governor

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:355`

## tutorial.46.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.46.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你可以指派§S总督§!管理整个§S星域§!。星域内的所有£planet£§S星球§!都将从总督的技能中受益。我们应当为我们的新星域指派一位总督。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

§SGovernors§! can be assigned to administrate entire §SSectors§!. All £planet£ §SPlanets§! within the sector will then benefit from the Governor's skills. We should assign one to our new sector.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:356`

## tutorial.47.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.47.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：指派总督

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

Tutorial: Governor Assigned

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:357`

## tutorial.47.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.47.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的新§S总督§!已经在新星域的首府开始办公了。干的好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Our new §SSector Governor§! has assumed office on the sector's capital world. Well done.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:358`

## tutorial.48.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.48.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：舰船设计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Tutorial: Ship Design

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:359`

## tutorial.48.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.48.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在可以在§S舰船设计器§!界面中亲手设计新的§S舰船§!了。这个过程比较直截了当，当然我们也可以进行全自动设计。\n\n访问§S舰船设计§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左侧边栏点击§S舰队管理§!按钮或按§SF9§!键\n\n$tttt$$tt$£fleet_management£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

We have the ability to manually design new §SShips§! in the §SShip Designer§! screen. This is a fairly straight-forward process, though one that can be fully automated if we so wish.\n\nTo access the §SShip Designer§!;\n\n£dot_green£Click the §SFleet Management§! button on the left side bar or press §SF9§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£fleet_management£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:360`

## tutorial.49.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.49.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：升级舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Tutorial: Upgrade Ship

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:361`

## tutorial.49.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.49.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

干的好。新的舰船设计已经添加到我们的数据库中，可以在装有§S船坞§!的§S太空港§!中进行生产。\n\n新设计可以在任何时候在§S舰船设计器§!中进行编辑。设计做出变动时，已建造的§S舰船§!会需要在§S恒星基地§!中进行升级。\n\n升级§S舰船§!；\n\n£dot_green£在§S总览§!中选择§S舰船§!\n£dot_green£在舰队界面点击§S升级§!按钮\n\n$tttt$$tt$£upgrade_ship£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Good job. The ship design has been added to our database, and can now be produced by a §SStarbase§! that has been equipped with a §SShipyard§!.\n\nThe new design can be edited at any time by revisiting the §SShip Designer§!. If any changes are made to the design, then §SShips§! already built will need to be upgraded at a §SStarbase§!.\n\nTo upgrade a §SShip§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select the §SShip§! in the §SOutliner§!\n£dot_green£ Click the §SUpgrade§! button in the fleet interface\n\n$tttt$$tt$£upgrade_ship£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:362`

## tutorial.52.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.52.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过研究，[From.GetName]上的异常现象是一种自然的§Y几何构造§!。看起来就像是一个指路的标记符号，宇宙的自然之美真是令人惊讶。\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

The anomaly on [From.GetName] turned out to be a natural §Ygeometrical formation§!. It almost looks like a marker, showing the way forward. The natural beauty of the universe can be quite astonishing.\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:363`

## tutorial.52.spirit.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.52.spirit.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]上发现了一片自然构造，其完美的几何结构令人窒息。这实乃上天的鬼斧神工，要引领我们到群星中去！\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

We found a natural formation on [From.GetName], stunning in its geometrical perfection. This is surely a divine sign, marking our way out towards the stars!\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:364`

## tutorial.52.filler.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.52.filler.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该§Y继续调查§!这些异常现象。这样一定会获得新的知识和科技的吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

We should §Ycontinue to investigate§! these sorts of anomalies whenever we get the chance. It will most assuredly grant us new knowledge and technology.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:365`

## tutorial.52.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.52.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真令人吃惊！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Astonishing!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:366`

## tutorial.52.spirit.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.52.spirit.a&lang=en)

### 中文

赞美奇迹！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Praised be the divine!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:367`

## tutorial.53.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]探测到的异常现象背后，发现一些十分惊人的东西。[From.GetPlanetMoon]周围环绕着的轨迹显然是超光速引擎留下的。由于其年代久远，我们无法进行追溯。\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

The anomaly we detected on [From.GetName] unveils something astonishing. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] is surrounded by a trail undoubtedly produced by FTL engines. We are unable to trace it to its origin, due to it being generated long ago.\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:368`

## tutorial.53.xenophobe.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.xenophobe.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]附近发现了微弱的超光速航道轨迹。其年代久远，业已无法进行追溯。这说明在很早之前就有星际旅行者来过这里，观察我们，收集情报。\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

We have detected a weak FTL trail near [From.GetName]. It is very old, making it impossible to track. This must mean that other interstellar travelers have been here before, watching us, surely gathering intel.\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:369`

## tutorial.53.xenophile.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.xenophile.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]上的异常现象显示了其他星际旅行者的痕迹！这些微弱的超光速航道轨迹表明，在很早之前，有人造访了我们£planet£§S行星§!寻求潜在的盟友。但是痕迹衰退严重，已无法进行追溯。\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

The anomaly on the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] shows traces of other interstellar travelers! A weak FTL trail shows that someone was here long ago, maybe watching our £planet£ §SPlanet§! for potential allies. It is too diminished to trace any further.\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:370`

## tutorial.53.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经被人观察？对方究竟有何目的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

We've been watched, but why?

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:371`

## tutorial.53.xenophobe.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.xenophobe.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须警惕异种的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

We must beware the xeno threat.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:372`

## tutorial.53.xenophile.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.53.xenophile.a&lang=en)

### 中文

去把他们找出来吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

Let's find them!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:373`

## tutorial.54.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.54.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]上找到了一个我方的§Y旧探测器§!。这些年来它一直保持运作，还试图将数据传送到母星上去。这是我们往日艰苦奋斗的纪念碑，也代表了昔日我们冲破§S行星§!枷锁的愿望。\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

We found one of our §Yold probes§! on [From.GetName]. It has somehow kept intact all these years and is still trying to send data to our homeworld. It stands as a testament to our past struggles, and our wish to flee our §SPlanet's§! bounds.\n\n$tutorial.52.filler.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:374`

## tutorial.54.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.54.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从未止步不前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

We are moving ever forward.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:375`

## tutorial.54.spiritualist.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.54.spiritualist.a&lang=en)

### 中文

神迹将继续震惊世人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

The divine surely has more in store.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:376`

## tutorial.54.materialist.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.54.materialist.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的努力终于有了回报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Our efforts have finally paid off.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:377`

## tutorial.55.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.55.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：战略资源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Tutorial: Strategic Resources

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:378`

## tutorial.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一种§S战略资源§!！许多£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!需要特定的科技才能利用，但可以从中获得强力的加成。\n\n收集§S战略资源§!；\n\n£dot_green£选中一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!\n£dot_green£右键点击包含该资源的£system£§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£点击§S[GetBuildGrowCap]£mining_station£开采站§!\n\n§S战略资源§!可以用于建造高级£building£§S建筑§!和舰船部件，也可以用来售卖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

We have uncovered a §SStrategic Resource§!! Many £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§! require specific technologies to exploit, but can confer powerful bonuses.\n\nTo secure a §SStrategic Resource§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!\n£dot_green£ Right-click the £system£ §SSystem§! containing the resource\n£dot_green£ Select §S[GetBuildGrowCap] £mining_station£ Mining Stations§!\n\n§SStrategic Resources§! can be used to build advanced £building£ §SBuildings§! and Ship components or simply sold.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:379`

## tutorial.55.poi.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.55.poi.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：资源位置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Tutorial: Resource Location

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:380`

## tutorial.55.poi.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.55.poi.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]上发现了§S战略资源§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

We have discovered a §SStrategic Resource§! on the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:381`

## tutorial.56.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.56.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：资源提取完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Tutorial: Resource Extracted

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:382`

## tutorial.56.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.56.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

干的好。这些资源会让我们帝国更加富裕的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Excellent work. This resource is sure to greatly enrich our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:383`

## tutorial.56.poi.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.56.poi.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[StrategicResourcePlanet.GetPlanetMoon][StrategicResourcePlanet.GetName]上发现了§S战略资源§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

We have discovered a §SStrategic Resource§! on the [StrategicResourcePlanet.GetPlanetMoon] [StrategicResourcePlanet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:384`

## tutorial.560.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.560.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现有的£mining_station£§S开采站§!在使用了新研发的科技之后就可以采集§S战略资源§!了。这些稀有资源会带来巨大的加成，同时也是极好的商品。真是一箭双雕啊！\n\n更多的信息可以在§S情报日志§!里找到。星系里还有更多的£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!等待我们去发现。继续探索吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Using recently-developed technology, an already-extant £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! can now mine a §SStrategic Resource§!. These rare resources confer powerful bonuses and make for valuable trade goods. Two metaphorical avians, allegorically struck down by an imaginary projectile.\n\nMore information can be found in the §SSituation Log§! for a time. There are many more £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§! left to discover in the galaxy, so keep on researching!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:385`

## tutorial.57.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.57.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：提升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Tutorial: Uplift

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:386`

## tutorial.57.desc.notech

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.57.desc.notech&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了高度进化的生命体！在正确§S科技§!的引领下，搞不好还可以进行§S提升§!，提升他们的自我知觉和智力——或者说，是有机体中被认为是智力的东西。\n\n时刻注意我们的研究才是明智之选。如果有机会的话，我们可以将这个种群向§S外源性刺激§!推进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

We have discovered highly-evolved animal life! With the right §STechnology§!, we may eventually be able to §SUplift§! these beings to sapience and intelligence - or what passes for intelligence among organics.\n\nIt would perhaps be wise to keep an eye on our research, and direct it towards §SEpigenetic Triggers§! if given the opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:387`

## tutorial.57.desc.yestech

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.57.desc.yestech&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了高度进化的动物生命！在正确§S科技§!的引领下，我们或许可以最终进行§H提升§!这些生命体的智慧和智力——或者说，是有机体中被认为是智力的东西。\n\n哦。\n\n§S基因表达学机制§!技术应该符合要求，我们是时候将这些生物提升到一个全新的境界了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

We have discovered highly-evolved animal life! With the right §STechnology§!, we may eventually be able to §HUplift§! these beings to sapience and intelligence - or what passes for intelligence among organics.\n\nOh.\n\nThe §SEpigenetic Triggers§! technology should fit the bill, and we are in fact ready to elevate these creatures into a brave new world of existential angst.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:388`

## tutorial.58.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.58.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：未开智物种已提升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Tutorial: Pre-Sapients Uplifted

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:389`

## tutorial.58.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.58.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真是大获成功！那些原始的野兽已经提升到不那么原始的级别，成为我们蓬勃发展的帝国的人口了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

A rousing success! The lowly beasts have been uplifted to become less lowly beasts, and are now proud citizens of our burgeoning empire.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:390`

## tutorial.59.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.59.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Tutorial: First Contact

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:391`

## tutorial.59.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.59.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

请注意！我们已经发现了拥有太空飞行能力的§S外星生物§!，他们是否具有智能尚不明确。不过，应该是有智能才对吧。我们必须完成§S调查§!才能揭开他们的神秘面纱。我建议我们先行行动，不要等到他们来调查我们……\n\n要调查§S第一次接触§!；\n\n£dot_green£打开§S情报日志§!\n£dot_green£选择§S$SHOW_FIRST_CONTACT$§!\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£firstcontact£\n\n§S第一次接触§!也可通过点击§S星系地图§!上的图标发起

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Remarkable! We have discovered §Sspacefaring alien entities§! that may or may not be intelligent, but, let's face it, probably are. Their true nature will remain a mystery to us until we complete the appropriate project to §SInvestigate§! them. I suggest we do so post-haste, lest they investigate us first...\n\nTo investigate a §SFirst Contact§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Open the §SSituation Log§!\n£dot_green£ Click on §S$SHOW_FIRST_CONTACT$§!\n\n$tttt$$ttt$£firstcontact£\n\n§SFirst Contact§! can also be initiated by clicking the icon on the §SGalaxy map§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:392`

## tutorial.61.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.61.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：未开智物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Tutorial: Pre-Sapient Species

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:395`

## tutorial.61.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.61.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们建立沟通的尝试代表了我们技术上的成熟，但结果并不理想。§S如果宇宙中还有其他智能生物的话§!，也许是些只具备动物水平智能的家伙吧。\n\n§S前智慧生物§!跟可没法搞什么外交，但取决于你的帝国类型，有时候可以对他们提升、灭绝、奴役或者进行观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Our attempts to establish communications stand testament to our technological ingenuity, but results are lacking. It is likely that the other party possesses only an animal level of intelligence, §Sif any at all§!.\n\n§SPre-Sapients§! offer little to no diplomatic interactions, but depending on your empire type, they can sometimes be uplifted, exterminated, enslaved, or observed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:396`

## tutorial.63.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.63.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：信号传输接入

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Tutorial: Incoming Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:397`

## tutorial.63.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.63.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看起来§S对方§!抢了我们的风头，在我们尚未解密他们的通讯模式前和我们§S建立了接触§!。\n\n在下次有这样的机会出现时，希望可以由我们来完成这一步吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

It appears the §Sother party§! completely stole our thunder, §Sestablishing contact§! with us before we had managed to decipher their communications protocols.\n\nWe should still attempt to accomplish this feat for ourselves, when the opportunity next presents itself.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:398`

## tutorial.64.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.64.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：修饰物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Tutorial: Modify Species

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:399`

## tutorial.64.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.64.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了近来遗传学的进步，我们已经获得了额外的§S特质点数§!，可以用来§S修饰§!和强化我们的人民了。\n\n修饰§S物种§!；\n\n£dot_green£打开§S物种§!选项卡或按§SF6§!键\n£dot_green£点击§S修饰§!按钮\n£dot_green£研究§S特殊项目§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Thanks to recent advances in genetics, we have gained an extra §STrait Point§! that we can use to §SModify§! and enhance our people.\n\nTo modify a §SSpecies§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select the §SSpecies§! tab or press §SF6§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the §SModify§! button\n£dot_green£ Research §SSpecial Project§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:400`

## tutorial.65.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.65.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：物种已修饰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Tutorial: Species Modified

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:401`

## tutorial.65.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.65.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真是太棒了。我们的帝国终于摆脱了自然进化的束缚，一定可以在宇宙中大展宏图了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

Commendable. With the yoke of natural evolution lifted from their shoulders, our people are sure to thrive in space.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:402`

## tutorial.70.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.70.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：超光速旅行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Tutorial: Warp Travel

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:403`

## tutorial.70.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.70.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了缩短£system£§S恒星系§!间的遥远距离，我们的£scientist£§S科学家§!们发明了§S曲速引擎§!。§S舰船§!装备了这种装置，就可以在两个星系中以超过光速的速度穿梭。\n\n虽然§S飞船§!可以通过曲速引擎到达范围内的星系，但是其只能在星系引力圈外围才能启动。§S舰船§!从超光速飞行状态中脱离后，需要一定时间补充能源，方可进行下一次星际旅行。过程的长度取决于§S舰船§!旅行的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

To overcome the vast distances that separate £system£ §SStar Systems§!, our £scientist£ §SScientists§! have developed the §SWarp Drive§!. §SShips§! equipped with this device can travel between systems at velocities exceeding that of lightspeed.\n\nAlthough §SShips§! can warp to any system within range, warp drives can only be activated outside the gravity well of a star. §SShips§! coming out of warp also require time to power up their systems again - the length of this process depends on how far the §SShip§! has traveled.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:404`

## tutorial.71.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.71.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：超空间旅行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Tutorial: Hyperspace Travel

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:405`

## tutorial.71.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.71.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了缩短£system£§S恒星系§!间的遥远距离，我们的£scientist£§S科学家§!们发明了§S超空间引擎§!。这种装置可以在超空间连接的两个恒星系间以远超光速的速度穿梭。\n\n虽然使用超空间进行移动速度极快，但这种旅行只能限制在星系超维平面网络上。\n\n要进入超空间，§S舰船§!必须先抵达恒星系边缘，到恒星重力井之外。\n\n当指挥§S舰船§!移动到其他§S恒星系§!时会自动进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

To overcome the vast distances that separate £system£ §SStar Systems§!, our £scientist£ §SScientists§! have developed the §SHyper Drive§!. This device permits travel at speeds far exceeding that of light between systems connected by hyperlanes.\n\nTravel along a hyperlane is nearly instantaneous, but movement is restricted to the galactic hyperlane network. \n\nTo enter hyperspace, a §SShip§! must first reach the system's edge, beyond the star's gravity well.\n\nThis happens automatically when ordering a §SShip§! to move to another §SSystem§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:406`

## tutorial.72.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.72.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：虫洞旅行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

Tutorial: Wormhole Travel

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:407`

## tutorial.72.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.72.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了缩短£system£§S恒星系§!间的遥远距离，我们的£scientist£§S科学家§!们发明了的§S虫洞§!技术。装备了虫洞发射器的空间站可以在空间中开辟通道，创造两个星系中暂时的连通渠道，允许§S舰船§!通过。\n\n虫洞旅行十分便捷，但需要范围内建立虫洞站。§S舰船§!也需要装备虫洞稳定器抵御穿过虫洞时的压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

To overcome the vast distances that separate £system£ §SStar Systems§!, our £scientist£ §SScientists§! have developed §SWormhole§! technology. Stations equipped with wormhole generators can tunnel through subspace, creating temporary gateways to other systems that §SShips§! can travel through.\n\nWormhole travel is near-instantaneous, but it requires the presence of a Wormhole Station within range. §SShips§! also need to be equipped with special wormhole stabilizers to withstand the stress of passing through subspace.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:408`

## tutorial.73.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.73.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：提升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Tutorial Reminder: Uplift

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:409`

## tutorial.73.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.73.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要有合适的技术，我们就有将低级生物§Y提升§!至开智种族的方法了。\n\n要提升§S物种§!；\n\n£dot_green£打开§S未开智§!£planet£§S行星§!\n£dot_green£选择§S管理§!选项卡\n£dot_green£选中§S物种§!\n£dot_green£点击§S设置权利§!\n£dot_green£点击§S提升§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Having researched the appropriate technology, we now have the means to §YUplift§! lesser beings to sapience.\n\nTo uplift a §SSpecies§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Open the §SPre-Sapient§! £planet£ §SPlanet§!\n£dot_green£ Click the §SManagement§! tab\n£dot_green£ Select the §SSpecies§!\n£dot_green£ Click on §SSet Rights§!\n£dot_green£ Click on §SUplift§!\n\nThe species will begin the uplift process, gaining full sapience once completed.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:410`

## tutorial.75.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.75.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：宣称恒星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

Tutorial: Claim System

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:411`

## tutorial.75.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.75.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经开始探索邻近的一些星系，或许是时候再将手伸远一些了。\n\n§S恒星基地§!可以在已调查的£system£§S恒星系§!中建造，用以拓展疆界，为我们获取新空间区域的所有权。\n\n有了更先进的科技后，恒星基地还可以升级并用于产出§S舰船§!或者资源。\n\n宣称§S恒星系§!；\n\n£dot_green£选择一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!\n£dot_green£右键点击一个已调查的§S恒星系§!\n£dot_green£选择§S[GetBuildGrowCap]恒星基地§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Now that we have begun exploring our neighboring stars, it might be time to extend our reach.\n\nA §SStarbase§! can be built around stars in surveyed £system£ §SSystems§! to expand our borders, allowing us to claim new regions of space\n\nWith future technologies, Starbases can be upgraded to produce §SShips§! or other resources.\n\nTo claim a §SSystem§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Select a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!\n£dot_green£ Right-click a surveyed §SSystem§!\n£dot_green£ Select §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Starbase§!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:412`

## tutorial.76.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.76.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：恒星基地升级

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Tutorial: Starbase Upgrade

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:413`

## tutorial.76.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.76.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很棒。你在§S星系地图§!中就能看到，我们帝国的疆界已经拓展到了这个刚刚建好§S恒星基地§!的£system£§S恒星系§!上了。\n\n新建的§S恒星基地§!只是一座哨站，只有非常有限的防御能力，没有安装§S模组§!和£building£§S建筑§!的空间。因此，这些没有升级过的哨站是不会显示在总览中的。\n\n升级§S恒星基地§!；\n\n£dot_green£在§S星系地图§!点击恒星系\n£dot_green£选中§S恒星基地§!\n£dot_green£点击§S升级§!按钮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Excellent. As you can see on the §SGalaxy Map§!, the borders of our empire have expanded to include the £system£ §SSystem§! where we built our new §SStarbase§!.\n\nA newly built §SStarbase§! acts as an Outpost, with limited defensive capabilities and no room for §SModules§! and £building£ §SBuildings§!. As such, it does not yet appear in the outliner until it has been upgraded.\n\nTo upgrade a §SStarbase§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the system on the §SGalaxy Map§!\n£dot_green£ Select the §SStarbase§!\n£dot_green£ Click on the §SUpgrade§! button

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:414`

## tutorial.80.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.80.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：地图模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

Tutorial: Map Modes

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:415`

## tutorial.80.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.80.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在宇宙地图中，可以在右下方打开和关闭不同的§S地图模式§!，以便浏览不同的信息种类。比如，可以根据各方势力对我们的看法进行排列，并用不同颜色标注在地图上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

On the Galaxy Map we can enable and disable different §SMap Modes§!, located in the bottom-right, to view various information overlays. For example, we can color-code territories according to their owners' opinion of us.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:416`

§HTutorial Missions§! and §HTips§! are enabled.
