## tutorial.1337.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1337.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：稀有文物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Tutorial: Minor Artifacts

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:425`

## tutorial.1337.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1337.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一些£minor_artifacts£§S稀有文物§!。稀有文物是一种深奥的资源，是一个用于描述我们不了解的、事实上可能无法解释的奇怪事物的笼统术语。\n\n它们可以用于执行各种不同的决议。\n\n使用§S稀有文物§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左侧边栏点击§S$topbar_button_discoveries_name$§!\n£dot_green£选择§S收藏§!选项卡或按§SC§!键\n\n$tttt$$tt$£discoveries£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

We have discovered some £minor_artifacts£ §SMinor Artifacts§!. These are an esoteric resource - a catch-all term for strange items which we do not understand and indeed may be inexplicable.\n\nThese can be used to enact a variety of decisions.\n\nTo interact with §SMinor Artifacts§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §S$topbar_button_discoveries_name$§! button on the left side bar\n£dot_green£ Select the §SCollection§! tab or press §SC§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£discoveries£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:426`

## tutorial.1340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$astral_threads$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Tutorial: $astral_threads$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:428`

## tutorial.1340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.1340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一些£astral_threads£§S星界丝缕§!它们是时空压缩的细丝，是维度位面碰撞的产物。通常情况下，这些丝线只能从§S星界细缝§!中获取，或者进入£astral_rift£§S星界裂隙§!中取回。\n\n执行各种§S星界行动§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左边栏点击§S$topbar_button_discoveries_name$§!按钮\n£dot_green£选择§S星界丝缕§!选项卡\n\n$tttt$$tt$£discoveries£\n\n你可以通过探索§S星界裂隙§!解锁更多的行动类型，这些裂隙可能从§S星界细缝§!或其他异常事件中出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

We have discovered some £astral_threads£ §SAstral Threads§!. These are filaments of condensed space-time: the result of dimensional planes colliding. Typically, these can only be harvested from §SAstral Scars§! or recovered inside £astral_rift£ §SAstral Rifts§!.\n\nTo perform various §SAstral Actions§!;\n\n£dot_green£ Click the §S$topbar_button_discoveries_name$§! button on the left side bar\n£dot_green£ Select the §SAstral Threads§! tab\n\n$tttt$$tt$£discoveries£\n\nYou can unlock additional actions by exploring §SAstral Rifts§!, which can emerge from §SAstral Scars§! or form through other anomalous events.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:429`

## tutorial.2000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进行第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Conducting First Contact

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:431`

## tutorial.2000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某些§S第一次接触§!会给出选项，让我方选择如何进行接触事项。这些选择也会长期地影响同其他帝国的外交关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Some §SFirst Contact§! missions will present options for how we wish to conduct matters. These choices can affect our diplomatic relationships with other empires for a long time.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:432`

## tutorial.2006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：外交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Tutorial: Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:433`

## tutorial.2006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯协议已经建立。我们现在可以同这个帝国展开§S外交§!。他们对我方的当前评价取决于我们在§S第一次接触§!时采取的行径。\n\n要展开§S外交§!；\n\n£dot_green£在左侧边栏点击§S通讯§!按钮或按§SF10§!键\n\n$tttt$$tt$£contacts£\n\n在第一次接触任务中，我们也获取了一些情报。随着我们逐渐掌握一个帝国的情报，更多有关他们的细节也将为我们所见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Communications protocols have been established. We can now engage in §SDiplomacy§! with this Empire. Their current opinion of us depends on how §SFirst Contact§! was conducted.\n\nTo engage in §SDiplomacy§!;\n\n£dot_green£Click the §SContacts§! button on the left side bar or press §SF10§!\n\n$tttt$$tt$£contacts£\n\nDuring first contact, we also gain Intel. As we gain further Intel on an empire, more details about them become visible to us.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:434`

## tutorial.2007.name

### 中文

$tutorial.2006.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

$tutorial.2006.name$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:435`

## tutorial.2007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与一种不同类型的文明进行了§S第一次接触§!——这类文明通常不愿与其他国家开展§S外交§!。\n\n能够与这类文明进行的外交行动十分有限，然而他们也有可能偶尔会决定主动接触我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

We have made §SFirst Contact§! with a different type of civilization - one which is generally unwilling to engage in §SDiplomacy§! with others.\n\nThe diplomatic actions we can take with civilizations like this are limited, however they may decide to contact us from time to time.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:436`

## tutorial.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：指派线人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Tutorial: Assigning Assets

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:437`

## tutorial.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§S线人§!泛指有价值的特工和装置，能为我方谍报活动提供不同效果。线人在间谍网内发挥作用，也可以指派到行动当中，以发掘新的机会或是帮助我们摆脱困境。\n\n我们可以通过事件或者执行§S$operation_acquire_asset$§!行动来得到线人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

§SAssets§! are valuable agents and artifacts which provide different effects for our Espionage activities. They work within the Spy Network and may be assigned to Operations, where they can open up new opportunities or else help us out of trouble.\n\nWe may receive Assets through events, or by performing the §S$operation_acquire_asset$§! Operation.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:438`

## tutorial.2100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：城邦外交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Tutorial: Enclave Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:440`

## tutorial.2100.abbr.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2100.abbr.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经与一个§S太空城邦§!进行了§S第一次接触§!。太空城邦是一种小型但独立的国家，可以与像我们这样的大型帝国开展贸易，并且外交形式也较为独特。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

We have made §SFirst Contact§! with an §SEnclave§!. This is a smaller, independent type of empire which may trade with larger empires like ours, using unique forms of Diplomacy.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:441`

## tutorial.2100.full.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2100.full.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$tutorial.2100.abbr.desc$\n\n太空城邦能够提供多种形式的服务：从协助科学研究到倒卖信息，从修建恒星基地建筑到贩卖§S舰船§!，应有尽有。购买城邦的服务将提升他们对你的§S评价§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

$tutorial.2100.abbr.desc$\n\nEnclaves offer a wide variety of services: from conducting scientific research to selling information, starbase buildings, and even §SShips§!. Purchasing services like these will increase that enclave's §SOpinion§! of us.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:442`

## tutorial.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tutorial.2100.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

Tutorial: $tutorial.2100.name$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:443`

## tutorial.2105.abbr.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2105.abbr.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们接触到了一个§S佣兵城邦§!。这些独立自主的军事专家会向各种国家，当然也包括我们国家，收费并租借§S佣兵舰队§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

We have encountered a §SMercenary Enclave§!. These independent military specialists may loan out §SMercenary Fleets§! to empires including our own - for a fee.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:444`

## tutorial.2105.full.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2105.full.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$tutorial.2105.abbr.desc$\n\n如果我们选择租借一支佣兵舰队，我们会在指定时间内获得舰队的控制权。在该时间段内，城邦会增补损失的舰船，但不论如何我们都无法将佣兵舰队极其海军司令与我方舰船合并。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

$tutorial.2105.abbr.desc$\n\nIf we choose to hire a Mercenary Fleet, we will retain control of it for a set period of time. During that time, the Enclave will attempt to reinforce the fleet when necessary, however neither a Mercenary Fleet nor its Admiral may be integrated with our own fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:445`

## tutorial.2111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：佣兵城邦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Tutorial: Mercenary Enclave

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:446`

## tutorial.2111.abbr.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2111.abbr.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们同意，§Y[tutorial_mercenary_fleet.GetName]§!马上可以建立§S佣兵城邦§!，舰船也将不再受我们控制，重新成为独立性军事力量，并且可以重新向包括我们在内的所有国家租借§S佣兵舰队§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

§Y[tutorial_mercenary_fleet.GetName]§! is now capable of becoming a §SMercenary Enclave§!, if we so wish. The fleet would cease to be under our direct control - instead it would become an independent force, capable of loaning out §SMercenary Fleets§! to empires including our own.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:447`

## tutorial.2111.full.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2111.full.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$tutorial.2111.abbr.desc$\n\n如果我们划出一片区域给佣兵城邦，那么只要它们把佣兵舰队租借给其他国家，我们就可以赚取§S收入§!。如果我们选择自己租借舰队，我们将在一定时间内保留对该舰队的控制权。另外如果雇佣舰队目前不可用，帝国需要额外支付一笔费用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

$tutorial.2111.abbr.desc$\n\nFor as long as we share space with the Mercenary Enclave, we stand to earn §Sincome§! whenever it loans its Mercenary Fleet out to other empires. If we choose to hire the Mercenary Fleet ourselves, we will retain control of that fleet for a set period of time. Empires may pay extra to buy out an existing contract if the Mercenary Fleet is not currently available.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:448`

## tutorial.2115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：巨型结构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Tutorial: Megastructures

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:449`

## tutorial.2115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£megastructure£§S巨型结构§!是一种我们可以在太空中[GetBuildGrow]的巨大工程。它们可以为我们提供顶尖的研究设施、海量的资源、甚至是一些独特的交通方式。\n\n建立一个巨型结构需要投入大量的时间和资源，但回报也同样可观。我们可以通过派遣一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!来开始修建巨型结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

£megastructure£ §SMegastructures§! are colossal projects that we can [GetBuildGrow] out in the void of space. They may provide us with cutting-edge research facilities, access to superior resources, or even some unique forms of transportation.\n\nBuilding on such a scale requires a significant investment of time and resources, but the rewards are every bit as sizable. We can get under way by setting a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! to this task.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:450`

## tutorial.2120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tech_orbital_ring_tier_1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Tutorial: $tech_orbital_ring_tier_1$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:451`

## tutorial.2120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于我们在巨型工程研究的进展，我们现在可以在宜居£planet£§S行星§!外围修建§S星环基地§!了。通过指派一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!以开始修建。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Thanks to our recent advances in mega-engineering research, we are now able to construct §SOrbital Rings§! around habitable £planet£ §SPlanets§!. We can begin building by issuing orders to a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:452`

## tutorial.2121.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2121.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$tutorial.2120.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

$tutorial.2120.name$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:453`

## tutorial.2121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§S星环基地§!是一种能够环抱整颗£planet£§S行星§!的£megastructure£§S巨型结构§!。它能搭建起一条支持空地间高效运转的通道，为诸如船坞、防御结构、资源仓库之类的的建筑提供空间。\n\n在星环基地界面我们可以修建或者移除£building£§S建筑§!或是§S模块§!，也可以升级星环基地至更高等级。升级基地可以增加可用的槽位数量，不过等级上限受到殖民地基础设施的限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

§SOrbital Rings§! are vast £megastructure£ §SMegastructures§! which are built to encircle an entire £planet£ §SPlanet§!. They make for an efficient bridge between space and the planet below, providing room for facilities like Shipyards, defensive structures, and resource hubs.\n\nThe Orbital Ring view is where we can add or remove £building£ §SBuildings§! and §SModules§!, as well as upgrade the Orbital Ring to higher tiers. Upgrading tiers increases the number of slots we have available to build in. Tiers are limited by colony infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:454`

## tutorial.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tech_quantum_catapult$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Tutorial: $tech_quantum_catapult$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:455`

## tutorial.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于我们在巨型工程研究的进展，我们现在可以在§S脉冲星§!或§S中子星§!周围修建§S量子弹弓§!了。通过指派一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!以开始修建。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

Recent advances in mega-engineering research mean we may now construct a §SQuantum Catapult§! around a §Spulsar§! or §Sneutron star§!. We can begin building by issuing orders to a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:456`

## tutorial.2126.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2126.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tutorial.2125.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Tutorial: $tutorial.2125.name$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:457`

## tutorial.2126.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2126.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§S量子弹弓§!能弹射§S舰船§!与舰队跨越遥远的天文尺度距离。该项科技极大地增强了我们应对帝国内部或是周边区域的应对能力。\n\n不过使用量子弹弓也有一定的风险，例如舰队有可能被弹射到预定目标以外的£system£§S恒星系§!。当然我们可以通过升级量子弹弓到更高等级，提高吞吐量与最大跃迁范围等属性，从而降低这些风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

The §SQuantum Catapult§! launches §SShips§! and fleets across vast, interstellar distances. This technology greatly improves our ability to react to emerging situations within our empire, and in nearby regions of space.\n\nUse of the Quantum Catapult carries some risk, as our fleets may arrive in £system£ §SSystems§! other than their intended target. We can mitigate these risks - and improve matters like throughput and maximum range - by upgrading the Quantum Catapult to higher tiers.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:458`

## tutorial.2130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tech_hyper_relays$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Tutorial: $tech_hyper_relays$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:459`

## tutorial.2130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于在§S超空间中继器§!方面的巨型工程研究已经产出了一定成果，我们可以着手指派一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!开始修建一个中继器网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Our mega-engineering research has yielded results in the form of §SHyper Relays§!. We can begin forming a network of these by issuing orders to a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:460`

## tutorial.2131.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2131.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：$tutorial.2130.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

Tutorial: $tutorial.2130.name$

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:461`

## tutorial.2131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§S超空间中继器§!能够加快在相邻£system£§S恒星系§!航行的速度。如果能够将§S至少两个§!超空间中继器链接到一起，那么我们就可以建立一个显著加快帝国内部航行速度的网络。\n\n超空间中继器也能与开放边境的相邻国家内部的中继器链接，从而将两国的网络相连。这将提高§S舰船§!穿越相邻国家的速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

§SHyper Relays§! allow for more efficient travel between adjacent £system£ §SStar Systems§!. By linking §Sat least two§! Hyper Relays together, we can begin to establish a network which will dramatically reduce transit times across our empire.\n\nHyper Relays may also be built adjacent to each other across open borders, linking whole networks together. This will allow §SShips§! to travel swiftly across neighboring empires.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:462`

## tutorial.2140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：观测站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Tutorial: Observation Posts

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:463`

## tutorial.2140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到了一个§S前超光速社会§!！虽然他们没有我们穿越星际的技术，但我们仍然可以从这些外星人身上得到很多东西。不论是学习他们的文化还是奴役他们，都取决于我们。\n\n如果计划观察他们（假定他们在我们的掌控范围内），我们需要在他们星球轨道上建立一座§S观测站§!。\n\n如果打算入侵他们，我们只需召集军队，他们没什么胜算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

We have detected a §SPre-FTL society§!! Although they lack the kind of technology that allows us to traverse the stars, there is still a lot to be gained from these aliens. Whether it be learning from their culture or claiming them as slaves is up to us.\n\nIf we wish to observe them - assuming they are within our borders - we need to build an §SObservation Post§! around their world.\n\nIf we wish to invade, all we need do is muster our troops; they wouldn't stand much of a chance.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:464`

## tutorial.2145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：观测站建造完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Tutorial: Observation Post Built

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:465`

## tutorial.2145.desc.nodlc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2145.desc.nodlc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的新§S观测站§!上线了。只要我们研究目标的£awareness£§S觉醒度§!保持低位，它就会继续执行§S任务§!。\n\n一旦前超光速社会的觉醒度达到上限，我们就无法再观察他们了。相反地，我们可以采取§S外交行动§!与他们接触。若§S干涉政策§!允许，我们也可提早现身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Our new §SObservation Post§! has come online and will proceed with its §SMission§! as long as the £awareness£ §SAwareness§! of the object of our study stays low.\n\nOnce a Pre-FTL society reaches maximum awareness, they will no longer be observable. Instead, we will be able to engage them with §SDiplomatic Actions§!. We may choose to reveal ourselves early if our §SInterference Policies§! allow it.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:466`

## tutorial.2145.desc.dlc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2145.desc.dlc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的新§S观测站§!上线了。只要我们研究目标的£awareness£§S觉醒度§!保持低位，它就会继续执行§S任务§!。\n\n我们能持续地从观测站获得一条独特的科技树，即§S观测洞见§!，直到这个前超光速社会的觉醒度达到上限而导致任务终止时。\n\n若是此类情况发生，我们可以取而代之采取§S外交行动§!与他们接触。若§S干涉政策§!允许，我们也可提早现身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

Our new §SObservation Post§! has come online and will proceed with its §SMission§! as long as the £awareness£ §SAwareness§! of the object of our study stays low.\n\nWe will gain §SObservation Insights§!, a unique line of technology, from this outpost until the Pre-FTL society reaches max awareness when missions will be disabled. Instead, we will be able to engage them with §SDiplomatic Actions§!.\n\nWe could choose to reveal ourselves early if our §SInterference Policies§! allow it.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:467`

### English

Tutorial: $tutorial.2006.name$

## tutorial.2150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个前超光速文明知晓了我们的存在。根据我们的§S政策§! 立场，我们目前能够采取有限的§S外交§!与§S行星§!的领袖接触。\n\n我们的存在已在星球上公开，也就没有继续观测的必要了。我们的§S观测任务§!已终止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

The pre-FTL civilization knows of our existence. We can now engage in limited §SDiplomacy§! with the §SPlanet's§! leadership, depending on our §SPolicy§! stance.\n\nWith our presence made public to the planet, there is no longer anything to gain from observing the civilization. Our §SObservation Missions§! have been disabled.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:469`

## tutorial.2500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：隐形力场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Tutorial: Cloaking Fields

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:471`

## tutorial.2500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

力场操纵研究领域的最新进展促使我们发明了§S$science_cloaking_components$§!。这些功率密集型部件可以投射出改良的粒子场，使我们的科研和军事舰船能够规避普通的传感器。\n\n然而，这些隐形场在激活时将对§S$MOD_SHIP_BASE_SPEED_MULT$§!、§S$MOD_SHIP_WINDUP_MULT$§!和§S$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_HP_ADD$§!产生负面影响。\n\n未被发现的§S隐形§!船只可以规避§S关闭边境§!导致的影响。\n在§S隐形§!状态下，我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!能够对他国的£colony£§S殖民地§!执行§S主动侦察§!，增加我们获得的关于其他帝国的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

Recent advances in Field Manipulation research have led to the invention of §S$science_cloaking_components$§!. These power-intensive components project modified particle fields allowing our science and military vessels to hide from conventional sensors.\n\nHowever, these cloaking fields will negatively affect §S$MOD_SHIP_BASE_SPEED_MULT$§!, §S$MOD_SHIP_WINDUP_MULT$§! and §S$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_HP_ADD$§! while active.\n\n§SCloaked§! ships that remain undetected can ignore §SClosed Borders§!.\nWhile §SCloaked§!, our £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! will be capable of conducting §SActive Reconnaissance§! on foreign £colony£ §SColonies§!, increasing the Intel we gain on other empires.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:472`

## tutorial.2550.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2550.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：隐形探测

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Tutorial: Cloaking Detection

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:474`

## tutorial.2550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial.2550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

计算机研究领域的最新进展促使我们发明了§S$sm_detection_array_plural$§!。在我们的§S恒星基地§!上安装这些高功率的外部传感器，将使我们有能力探测到§S隐形§!舰队。\n\n通过将多个阵列耦合在一起，我们可以提高§S$sm_detection_array_plural$§!的灵敏度，使它们能够穿透越来越稳定的§S隐形力场§!。\n\n随着进一步研究，我们应该能够利用£science_ship£§S科研船§!来进一步提高这些探测能力。这些设备的范围受限于恒星基地的§S探测范围§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Recent advances in Computing research have led to the invention of §S$sm_detection_array_plural$§!. Installing these high-powered external sensors on our §SStarbases§! will grant the ability to detect §SCloaked§! fleets.\n\nThe sensitivity of the §S$sm_detection_array_plural$§! can be increased by coupling multiple arrays together, allowing them to penetrate increasingly stable §SCloaking Fields§!.\n\nWith further research, we should be able to use £science_ship£ §SScience Ships§! to further increase boost these detection capabilities. The range of these devices is limited by the starbase's §SSensor Range§!.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:475`

## tutorial_survey_system_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_survey_system_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们探索繁星之前我们需要先拓宽视野。幸好，我们有一张复杂的§S星系地图§!可用。要打开星系地图，你可以按§SM§!键或是屏幕底部的星系按钮。之后就可以按§SEsc§!或是右上角的按钮关闭情报日志了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Before we can explore the stars we need to broaden our view. Thankfully, we have a sophisticated §SGalaxy Map§! at our disposal. To access the Galaxy Map, you can either press §SM§! or the Galaxy Button at the bottom of your screen. Afterwards feel free to close the Situation Log by either pressing §SEsc§! or the button in the top right.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:481`

## tutorial_1_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_1_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

首先选中我们的£science_ship£§S科研船§!。你可以在§H星系地图§!上直接点击§S舰船§!或是从§S总览§!中选中。\n\n然后在察看§S星系地图§!的同时，在左下角的舰队界面点击§S调查§!按钮，然后选择一个没有调查过的£system£§S恒星系§!。你也可以右键点击一个未调查的恒星系然后选择§S调查恒星系§!。\n\n我们的§S科研船§!会自动对那个恒星系内的所有天体进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

Begin by selecting our £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!. This can be done by clicking on the §SShip§! on the §HGalaxy Map§! or by selecting it from the §SOutliner§!.\n\nWhile viewing the §SGalaxy Map§!, click the §SSurvey§! button on the fleet interface in the lower left corner, then select a non-surveyed £system£ §SSystem§!. Alternatively, you can right-click on a non-surveyed system and select §SSurvey System§!.\n\nOur §SScience Ship§! will now chart a course and survey all planetary bodies within that system.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:485`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_unpause_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当£science_ship£§S科研船§!已经准备探索星系时，所需要做的只有取消暂停了。只需要简单地按下§S空格键§!£space£或在顶部栏点击以下按钮即可：\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$t$£pause_button£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

With a £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! standing by to explore the galaxy, all that is left is to unpause. To do this, simply press the §SSpace Bar§! £space£ or select the following icon in the top bar:\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$t$£pause_button£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:488`

## tutorial_2_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_2_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要研发新科技。§S新世界协议§!将使我们可以建造£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，如果我们要扩张自己的帝国，那我们应该尽快研发它。\n\n点击顶部界面左端的按钮或是按下§SF4§!键来开启§S科技§!界面。然后点击§S选择研究§!并从中挑选一项科技作为研究目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

We need to research new technologies. The §SNew Worlds Protocol§! will enable us to construct £colony_ship£ §SColony Ships§!, and we should focus on this as soon as possible if we are to expand our empire.\n\nAccess the §STechnology§! screen by clicking its button in the left section of the top bar or by pressing §SF4§!. Then click on §SSelect Research§! and pick a technology to pursue from the options available.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:493`

## tutorial_3_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_3_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果您想要开发被£science_ship£§S科研船§!发现的资源，那么您需要派遣£construction_ship£§S工程船§!执行相关任务。\n\n点击恒星基地选择§S船坞§!界面。再在几个选项中选择§S工程船§!。\n\n注意您需要足够的£alloys£§S合金§!才能开始建造这些§S舰船§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

We will need a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! if we are to exploit the resources that are discovered by our £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!.\n\nClick on our Starbase and select the §SShipyard§! tab, then select §SConstruction Ship§! from among the available choices.\n\nNote that you will need to have amassed enough £alloys£ §SAlloys§! to afford the §SShip§! before its construction can begin.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:498`

## tutorial_4_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_4_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

想开发£planet£§S行星§!上的£minerals£§S矿物§!或者§S战略资源§!矿藏，需要在轨道上建立£mining_station£§S开采站§!。为此，你需要选择£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，可以直接点击工程船或者在§S总览§!选择。\n\n选中之后，在工程船的舰队界面点击§S[GetBuildGrowCap]开采站§!按钮，然后点击拥有矿藏的可用行星或小行星。\n\n注意，建造£mining_station£§S开采站§!会花费£minerals£§S矿物§!资源，所以在建造前一定要确保拥有足够的矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

Before a £minerals£ §SMineral§! deposit or a §SStrategic Resource§! can be collected from a £planet£ §SPlanet§!, a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! needs to be built in orbit. To do this, select your £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! either by clicking directly on it or by finding it in the §SOutliner§! on the right side of the screen.\n\nOnce this has been done, click on the §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Mining Station§! button in the Construction Ship's fleet interface. Then click on an available planet or asteroid with a Mineral deposit.\n\nNote that building a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! also costs £minerals£ §SMinerals§!, so you will need to have a sufficient amount stored before construction can begin.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:502`

## tutorial_5_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_5_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了提升我们研究新技术的速度，请在拥有£physics£§S物理§!，£society£§S社会§!或£engineering£§S工程§!资源的行星上建立£science_ship£§S研究站§!。\n\n研究站的工作方式和£mining_station£§S开采站§!类似，其区别在于收集的资源不一样。研究站无法收集£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!和£minerals£§S矿物§!。\n\n要建造研究站，需要选择一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，并在舰队界面选择§S[GetBuildGrowCap]研究站§!的按钮，然后点击含有拥有物理、社会或工程资源的£planet£§S行星§!、小行星或恒星之上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

To improve the rate at which we research new technologies, £science_ship£ §SResearch Stations§! should be built above any world that possesses £physics£ §SPhysics§!, £society£ §SSociety§! or £engineering£ §SEngineering§! resources.\n\nResearch Stations function much in the same way as £mining_station£ §SMining Stations§! - their sole difference is in the resources they collect. They are not capable of mining £minerals£ §SMinerals§! or £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resources§!.\n\nTo build one, select your £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§! and click on the §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Research Station§! button in its fleet interface. Then click on an available £planet£ §SPlanet§!, asteroid or sun that has a deposit of Physics, Society or Engineering resources.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:506`

## tutorial_6_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_6_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的母星已经有少量£building£§S建筑§!，但我们应该继续建造。\n\n要完成这项任务，首先在§S总览§!点击我们的母星，点击§S地表§!选项卡。在一个区划中点击一个空白建筑槽，并从可用建筑中选择。只要我们拥有足够§S资源§!，建造就会立即开始。\n\n建筑提供£job£§S岗位§!，但在有£pops£§S人口§!被委派上去工作之前不会有任何产出。你可以在§S经济§!选项卡中查看你的§S人口§!都在从事什么岗位的工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Our homeworld already has a small number of £building£ §SBuildings§!, but we should build another one.\n\nTo do this, select the homeworld in the §SOutliner§! and click on the §SSurface§! tab. Select an empty building slot in a district, and choose from one of the available buildings. Assuming we have enough §SResources§!, construction will begin immediately.\n\nBuildings provide £job£ §SJobs§!, but nothing will be produced until £pops£ §SPops§! are assigned to work there. You can check which jobs your §SPops§! are performing in the §SEconomy§! tab.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:510`

## tutorial_7_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_7_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们母星上的一些=£blocker£§S障碍§!妨碍了§S区划§!的进一步开发，罪当清除。这就需要花费§S资源§!。\n\n要清理§S障碍§!，在§S管理§!选项卡中找到障碍，然后点击其上的§S清理障碍按钮§!。\n\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$tt$£clear_blocker£\n\n\n清理不同的§S障碍§!需要的花费和时间也有所不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

Several £blocker£ §SBlocker§! on our homeworld are preventing the construction of additional §SDistricts§!, and should be cleared. This takes time and costs §SResources§!.\n\nTo clear a §SBlocker§!, click the §SClear Blocker§! button on the blocker itself, which can be found on the §SManagement§! tab.\n\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$tt$£clear_blocker£\n\n\n§SBlockers§! have different costs, technology requirements, and times associated with clearing them.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:514`

## tutorial_8_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_8_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要在£system£§S母星系§!外扩张我们的帝国。首先要做的就是调查邻近星系。\n\n要进行探索，请选择你的£science_ship£§S科研船§!，在舰队界面点击§S调查§!按钮，然后点击屏幕下方的§S打开星系地图§!按钮，选择一个邻近星系。§S科研船§!会自动前往该星系，调查其中的全部天体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

We need to expand our influence beyond our home £system£ §SSystem§!, and the first step towards doing this is to survey the neighboring star systems.\n\nTo do this, select your £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! and click on the §SSurvey§! button in its fleet interface. Then click on the §SOpen Galaxy Map§! button at the bottom of the screen, and select one of the stars neighboring our own. The §SScience Ship§! will now journey to this star system and survey all bodies within.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:518`

## tutorial_9_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_9_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该考虑[GetBuildingGrowing]第二艘£science_ship£§S科研船§!来加速星系的探索进度。\n\n点击恒星基地选择§S船坞§!选项卡。再在几个选项中选择§S科研船§!。\n\n注意您需要足够的£alloys£§S合金§!才能开始建造这艘§S舰船§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

We should consider [GetBuildingGrowing] a second £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§! to increase the speed at which we survey our region of the galaxy.\n\nClick on our Starbase and select the §SShipyard§! tab, then select §SScience Ship§! from among the available choices.\n\nNote that you will need to have amassed enough £alloys£ §SAlloys§! to afford the §SShip§! before its construction can begin.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:524`

## tutorial_10_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_10_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们或许应当考虑雇佣一名新的£scientist£§S科学官§!来指挥全新的£science_ship£§S科研船§!。虽然科研船可以在没有科学家的情况下继续探索，但是失去了科学家的指导，他们便无法探测到§S异常现象§!。\n\n选择我们的新科研船，点击§S指派领袖§!。§S领袖§!页面会展开，显示为我们服务的所有科学家。在列表顶部点击§S招募§!按钮。\n\n选择你想要的科学家，然后点击§S招募§!按钮。招募领袖会花费£unity£§Y凝聚力§!，请务必储存足够。\n\n招募完毕后，新的科学家便会在母星中等待安排。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

We need a £scientist£ §SScientist§! to command our new £science_ship£ §SScience Ship§!, as it cannot survey or research §SAnomalies§! without one.\n\nSelect our new §SScience Ship§!, and then click on the portrait in the fleet interface. The §SSelect a Leader§! window will open, showing all §SScientists§! currently employed by us and those available for hire. Select the candidate you want.\n\nRecruiting a Leader costs £unity£ §YUnity§!, and you may need to wait until you have enough stored.\n\nOnce recruited, the §SScience Ship§! can begin to explore.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:528`

## tutorial_11_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_11_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在该殖民另一颗星球了。首先我们得[GetBuildGrow]一艘£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!。[GetBuildGrow]这种§S舰船§!需要花很长时间，以及大量的£alloys£§S合金§!、£food£§S食物§!和£consumer_goods£§S消费品§!，但兴旺发展的£colony£§S殖民地§!带来的回报远远大于这笔投入。\n\n点击你的§S恒星基地§!，打开§S船坞§!选项卡。然后从各种选项中点击选择§S殖民船§!。\n\n请注意，在建造开始之前，你必须先积累足够的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

The time has come to colonize another world. First we will have to [GetBuildGrow] a £colony_ship£ §SColony Ship§!. These §SShips§! take a long time to [GetBuildGrow] and require a large amount of £alloys£ §SAlloys§!, £food£ §SFood§!, and £consumer_goods£ §SConsumer Goods§!, but a thriving £colony£ §SColony§! will more than return on this investment.\n\nClick on your §SStarbase§! and open the §SShipyard§! tab. Then select §SColony Ship§! from the available designs.\n\nYou'll need enough resources before construction can begin.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:532`

## tutorial_12_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_12_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在我们该用新造的£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!来设立我们的第一个£colony£§S殖民地§!。最佳的候选星球是与我们的母星类型相同的星球，这样我们的殖民者在适应环境方面不会有任何问题。\n\n要殖民一颗£planet£§S行星§!，点击你的§S殖民船§!，或者在§S总览窗口§!中选中它。然后在其舰队界面中点击§S殖民§!按钮，并选择你想殖民的星球。注意你只能在已调查并且没有§S异常点§!的星球上殖民。星球也必须位于你的境内。虽然你可以选择在宜居性较低的星球上殖民，但代价会非常高昂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

We should now use our new £colony_ship£ §SColony Ship§! to establish our first £colony£ §SColony§!. The best planetary candidates are those that are of the same planet class as our homeworld - our colonists will have no problems adjusting to their environments.\n\nTo colonize a £planet£ §SPlanet§!, click on your §SColony Ship§! or select it in the §SOutliner§!. Then click on the §SColonize§! button in its fleet interface, and select the planet you want to colonize. Note that you can only colonize planets that have been surveyed and have no §SAnomalies§!. They must also be located within your borders. While you can choose to colonize low Habitability planets, doing so can be very expensive.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:536`

## tutorial_13_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_13_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要招募一名§S总督§!来管理我们新的£colony£§S殖民地§!。要进行招募，可点击殖民地，进入到§S殖民地总览§!标签中，在§S总督§!窗口点击§S更换§!按钮。此时会展开一个§S领袖§!窗口，显示现在为我们工作的总督。\n\n在屏幕上方点击§S招募§!按钮，选择一名可供使用的总督。注意，招募领袖会花费£unity£§Y凝聚力§!。如果储备不足，就要等到收集足够后再进行招募。\n\n新总督招募完毕后将会在母星等待安排。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

We should recruit a §SGovernor§! to administer our new £colony£ §SColony§!. To do this, click on the colony and go into its §SColony Summary§! tab. Then click on the §SChange§! button in the §SGovernor§! window. This will open the §SLeaders§! screen, showing the Governors currently working for us.\n\nClick on the §SRecruit§! button in the upper portion of the screen, and select from among the available candidates. Note that Leaders always cost £unity £§YUnity§! to recruit. If we lack the needed amount, we will have to wait until enough has been generated to afford the leader.\n\nOnce recruited, the new Governor will be awaiting assignment on our homeworld.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:540`

### English

## tutorial_14_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_14_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

以建立£colony£§S殖民地§!的方式继续扩张我们的帝国。我们有充足的£alloys£§S合金§!、£food£§S食物§!和£consumer_goods£§S消费品§!时，就[GetBuildGrowCap]更多£colony_ship£§S殖民船§!，然后让它们去建立更多殖民地。\n\n小心，不要在殖民地发展阶段同时建立过多的殖民地，因为他们能够自己自足之前一直会消耗£energy£§S能量币§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Continue to expand our empire by founding new £colony£ §SColonies§!. [GetBuildGrowCap] more £colony_ship£ §SColony Ships§! when we have enough £alloys£ §SAlloys§!, £food£ §SFood§!, and £consumer_goods£ §SConsumer Goods§! then send them off to establish new colonies.\n\nTake care not to have too many colonies under development at the same time, since they drain £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§! until they become self-sufficient.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:544`

教程：资源生产

### English

## tutorial_15_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_15_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不断成长的工业渴求原料，因此必须增加£minerals£§S矿物§!的产量。我们可以在有矿物储藏的£planet£§S行星§!和小行星上[GetBuildingGrowing]£mining_station£§S开采站§!，或是在某个已殖民的星球表面上建造§S采矿区划§!。\n\n空间站和£building£§S建筑§!都需要£energy£§S能量币§!来维护，因此我们也需要保证这项资源的充足供应。能量币由已殖民星球上的§S发电区划§!生产，也可以通过在有能量币储藏的行星、小行星和恒星上建造§S开采站§!来生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Our growing industry is ravenous for raw materials, and we need to increase our supply of £minerals£ §SMinerals§!. This is done by [GetBuildingGrowing] £mining_station£ §SMining Stations§! above £planet£ §SPlanets§! and asteroids with Mineral deposits, or by building §SMining Districts§! on the surface of one of our inhabited planets.\n\nAs both stations and £building£ §SBuildings§! cost £energy£ §SEnergy Credits§! to maintain, we need to establish an ample supply of this resource as well. Energy Credits are produced by §SGenerator Districts§! on the surface of our inhabited planets. They can also be acquired by §SMining Stations§! built over planets, asteroids or suns with Energy Credit deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:548`

## tutorial_16_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_16_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须[GetBuildGrow]一支强大的星际舰队，以确保[Root.GetName]的安全。\n\n目前来看，我们需要再[GetBuildGrowCap]几艘§S护卫舰§!。等到§S合金§!储量足够的时候，我们可以在§S恒星基地§!中进行。新造的军舰会自动并入位于建造它的§S恒星基地§!轨道上现有的舰队。\n\n为了最大化舰队的战斗力，我们可以为其指派一位§S舰队司令§!。这会让这支舰队在面对那些没有£commander£§S指挥官§!管辖的舰队时更具优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

We should [GetBuildGrow] a potent starfleet to uphold the [Root.GetName].\n\nA few more §SCorvettes§! should do, for now. [GetBuildGrowCap] the §SShips§! at a §SStarbase§! when our §SAlloy§! supplies permit. New military ships always join any existing fleets in orbit of the §SStarbase§! that builds them.\n\nTo optimize a fleet's military power, we may choose to assign it an §SAdmiral§!. This would give it an edge over other fleets, most of which serve without a £commander£ §SCommander§! of their own.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:553`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:557`

## tutorial_17_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_17_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您可以直接控制的£planet£§S行星§!数量是有上限的。这个上限显示在屏幕顶部，而超出上限将会带来负面影响。§S星域§!是半自治的，并会自动管理其下属的行星。要创建一个新星域，请点击屏幕顶部的下拉菜单，或是按下§SF5§!来进入§S行星和星域§!界面。然后点击右上角的§S创建新星域§!按钮。\n\n您将会进入§S星域管理模式§!。在这里您可以决定新星域将由哪些£system£§S恒星系§!组成。这些星系中的所有行星都将被纳入星域的管辖范围。选择一个拥有我方£colony£§S殖民地§!的星系，然后点击§S完成§!按钮。您将建立我们的第一个星域！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

There is a limit to how many £planet£ §SPlanets§! you can control directly. This limit can be found in the top bar, and going above it will incur penalties. §SSectors§! are semi-autonomous and manage the planets that have been assigned under their control. To create a new sector, go into the §SPlanets and Sectors§! view by clicking on its associated icon in the top bar drop-down list or press §SF5§!. Then click on the §SCreate New Sector§! button to the upper right.\n\nYou will now enter the §SSector Management Mode§!. From here, you designate which £system£ §SStar Systems§! should make up the new sector. All planets within those systems will fall under the sector's jurisdiction. Select a system where we have a £colony£ §SColony§!, and click on the §SFinish§! button. You will now have created our first sector!

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:558`

## tutorial_18_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_18_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该指派一位§S总督§!来管理我们的新§S星域§!。星域中的所有£planet£§S行星§!将受益于总督的能力等级与特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

We should assign a §SGovernor§! to administrate our new §SSector§!. All £planet£ §SPlanets§! within the sector will then benefit from the effects of the Governor's skill level and traits.

### English

## tutorial_19_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_19_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

要设计一种新的舰船，在侧边栏打开§S舰队管理§!或按§SF9§!后，点击打开§S舰船设计器§!。点击§S新设计§!按钮，在选择§S舰船尺寸§!时选择§S护卫舰§!。点击§S无区段§!按钮为该设计选择一个§S区段§!。\n\n护卫舰是最小型的战舰，它只有一个区段。更大型的舰船区段更多。区段的种类决定了§S武器§!和§S通用§!槽位。选择一个区段后，用各种不见填满，并注意保证能源供给虫族。\n\n通过点击§S自动装配§!可以简化该流程，让游戏自动完成设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

To create a new ship design, go into the §SShip Designer§! by clicking on the §SFleet Management§! in the left side bar, or press §SF9§!. Click on the §SNew Design§! button, and select §SCorvette§! when you are prompted to select a §SHull Size§!. Click the §SNo Section§! button to select a §SSection§! for the design.\n\nCorvettes, the smallest warships, have only one section, while larger ships have more. Sections determine §SWeapon§! and §SUtility§! slots. Pick one, then fill the slots with components, ensuring power remains positive.\n\nTo simplify the process, click §SAuto-complete Ship§! and let the game finalize the design.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:566`

### English

## tutorial_20_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_20_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该在一个§S[Owner.GetPreFTLUpper]社会§!的母星上方[GetBuildGrow]§S观测站§!。这些空间站可以完成多种不同的§S任务§!，而任务的种类则取决于我们想要如何影响这些发展中的文明。\n\n观测站的建造方式与£mining_station£§S开采站§!和£science_ship£§S科研站§!相同，但只能造在[Owner.GetPreFTLUpper]文明的母星上方。只需要选择一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，然后在其舰队界面中点击§S[GetBuildGrowCap]观测站§!。\n\n接下来选择你想要研究的[Owner.GetPreFTLUpperPlural]文明的母星，工程船就会自动开始建造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

We should [GetBuildGrow] an §SObservation Post§! above the homeworld of a §S[Owner.GetPreFTLUpper] society§!. These space stations can perform a number of different §SMissions§!, although our willingness to interfere with developing cultures will determine which ones are available.\n\nObservation Posts are built in the same way as £mining_station£ §SMining§! or £science_ship£ §SResearch Stations§!, but they can only be constructed over the homeworlds of [Owner.GetPreFTLUpper] Civilizations. Simply select a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!, then click on the §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Observation Post§! button in its fleet interface.\n\nNow select the homeworld of the [Owner.GetPreFTLUpperPlural] you wish to study, and the Construction Ship will begin its mission.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:570`

## tutorial_21_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_21_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该在拥有£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!的天体轨道上[GetBuildGrow]一个£mining_station£§S开采站§!。这些稀有的原料拥有巨大的战略价值。\n\n通过§S调查§!找到这类资源后，选取一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，然后选择§S[GetBuildGrowCap]开采站§!，然后点击相应的天体，即可进行建造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

We should [GetBuildGrow] a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! in orbit of an astronomical object with a £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resource§!. These rare materials have immense strategic value.\n\nHaving located such a resource through §SSurveying§!, select a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!, click on the §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Mining Station§! button and then on the appropriate astronomical object.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:574`

## tutorial_21b_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_21b_chain_title&lang=en)

## tutorial_21b_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_21b_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在一个有£strategic_resource£§S战略资源§!的天体的轨道上建造了一个£mining_station£§S开采站§!。这些需要前置科技才能开采的资源能带来巨大的战略价值，我们要一直去搜寻它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

We have built a £mining_station£ §SMining Station§! in orbit of an astronomical object with a £strategic_resource£ §SStrategic Resource§!. These rare materials require specific technologies to mine have immense strategic value, and we should always be on the lookout for them.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:576`

## tutorial_22_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_22_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该§S提升§!一个低等物种，不过首先我们需要研究§S基因表达学机制§!科技。\n\n§S提升§!未开智的物种，可以访问§S物种§!界面并选中该物种，然后点击提升按钮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

We should §SUplift§! a lesser species, though we may need to research the §SEpigenetic Triggers§! technology first.\n\n§SUplift§! a pre-sapient species by selecting their entry in the §SSpecies§! screen, and then clicking on the Uplift button.

## tutorial_23_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_23_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在宇宙空间的黑暗之中与未知的存在遭遇了，最好尝试着§S建立通讯§!。\n\n可以通过在§S情报日志§!中选择对应的§S第一次接触§!项目，点击§S显示第一次接触§!按钮，或者在§S星系地图§!上点击§S第一次接触§!图标来直接访问。\n\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$ttt$£firstcontact£\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Having encountered unknown entities in the blackness of space, we should attempt to §Sestablish communications§!. \n\nThis can be achieved by selecting §SFirst Contact§! in the §SSituation Log§! and clicking the §SShow First Contact§! button, or alternatively, by clicking the §SFirst Contact§! icon on the §SGalaxy Map§! to access it directly.\n\n\n$tttt$$tttt$$ttt$£firstcontact£\n

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:584`

## tutorial_24_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_24_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以§S修饰§!住在我们帝国的任何开智种族，如果他们有足够的§S特质点数§!的话。\n\n通过§S物种§!界面的§S修饰§!菜单，然后研究§S特殊项目§!。这个项目需要£society£§S社会学研究§!，并且在执行项目时会暂停正在进行的社会学研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

We can §SModify§! any sapient species living within our empire, provided they have enough §STrait Points§!.\n\nThis can be achieved by accessing the §SModify§! menu from the §SSpecies§! screen, and then researching the appropriate §SSpecial Project§!. The project requires £society£ §SSociety research§! and will divert all our technological progress in that field while it is active.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:588`

## tutorial_25_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atutorial_25_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以通过在外星球轨道上建造[GetBuildingGrowing]§S恒星基地§!来扩大我们的领土。这类前哨的存在宣示了我们对这颗恒星及其周围£system£§S星系§!的控制，也就意味着我们有权对其资源进行采集，并禁止已有过接触的外星种群随便进入。\n\n选择一艘£construction_ship£§S工程船§!，在舰队界面上点击§S[GetBuildGrowCap]恒星基地§!，然后左键点击一颗恒星。或者可以右键点击恒星然后点击§S[GetBuildGrowCap]恒星基地§!。\n\n与其他轨道站不同的是，§S恒星基地§!可以在我们的边境之外建造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

We can expand our borders by [GetBuildingGrowing] §SStarbases§! around alien stars. This secures the £system£ §SSystem§!, grants resource access, and blocks passage for contacts if borders are closed.\n\nSelect a £construction_ship£ §SConstruction Ship§!, then click the §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Starbase§! button in its fleet interface and left-click a star. Alternatively, right-click the star and select §S[GetBuildGrowCap] Starbase§!.\n\nUnlike other orbital stations, §SStarbases§! can be built outside of our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:592`

## TUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_MANAGEMENT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_PLANETVIEW_MANAGEMENT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S管理§!选项卡展示了关于£planet£§S行星§!状态和布局的关键信息。\n\n§S行星特征§!部分显示可以清理的天然£blocker£§S障碍§!以及所存在的§S特征§!。\n\n§S当前人口§!部分显示£planet£§S行星§!上的所有£pops£§S人口§!。\n\n£sector£§S星域面板§!可以用于将行星组织进§S星域§!，以此自动化殖民地管理。\n\n§S殖民地飞升§!可以升级殖民地，只要必须资源足够就可以有效地优化§S行星§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

The §SManagement§! tab displays key information about a £planet£ §SPlanet's§! condition and layout.\n\nThe §SPlanetary Features§! section shows natural £blocker£ §Sblockers§! that can be cleared, as well as existing §SFeatures§!.\n\nThe §SCurrent Population§! section shows all £pops£ §SPops§! on the £planet£ §SPlanet§!.\n\nThe £sector£ §SSector§! panel lets the planet be managed as part of a §SSector§!, automating colony administration.\n\n§SColony Ascension§! allows you to ascend your colonies, effectively upgrading your §SColonies§!, if the necessary resources are available.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:596`

## TUTORIAL_DATABANK_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_DATABANK_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

£databank£§S数据库§!是帮助你理解游戏系统的十万个为什么。\n\n其中的条目分门别类，便于你在任何时候查找特定的机制、功能或概念。\n\n通过直接搜索或者选择一个主题详细了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

The £databank£ §SDatabank§! is your go-to resource for understanding the game's systems.\n\nEntries are organized by category, making it easy to look up specific mechanics, §SFeatures§!, or concepts whenever you need.\n\nSimply search, or select a topic from the list to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:598`

## TUTORIAL_EMPIRE_FOCUS_TIMELINE_TITLE

### 中文

教程：焦点与时间线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Tutorial: Focus & Timeline

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:599`

## TUTORIAL_EMPIRE_FOCUS_TIMELINE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_EMPIRE_FOCUS_TIMELINE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

£focus_core£§S帝国焦点§!系统可以通过设置优先任务来引导你帝国的发展；£focus_exploration£§S探索§!、£focus_conquest£§S征服§!、以及£focus_development£§S发展§!。\n\n每个焦点包含了一组任务卡，为你所选择的道路指明方向。完成任务卡可以获得相关分类的§S进展分§!。\n\n当前的焦点将提高抽取到相关任务的概率。可以花费£unity£§S凝聚力§!重新抽取卡片，焦点也可以改变。\n\n在§S已完成§!和§S进展§!选项卡中追踪目标，可以查看距离下一个奖励等级还有多远。\n\n§S时间线§!展示了帝国历史上从§S起源§!开始的各种里程碑和重要历史事件。新的条目会随着事件的展开而逐渐出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

The £focus_core£ §SEmpire Focus§! system helps guide your empire's development by letting you choose a priority: £focus_exploration£ §SExploration§!, £focus_conquest£ §SConquest§!, or £focus_development£ §SDevelopment§!.\n\nEach focus includes a set of task cards, designed to offer direction based on your chosen path. Completing a card grants §SProgression Points§! toward the relevant category.\n\nThe current focus increases the chances of drawing related tasks. Cards can be rerolled for a £unity£ §SUnity§! cost, and focus can be changed.\n\nTrack your goals under the §SCompleted§! and §SProgression§! tabs to see how close you are to the next reward tier.\n\nThe §STimeline§! displays major milestones and historical events from your empire's history, starting from its §SOrigin§!. New entries appear as key events unfold over time.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:600`

### 中文

教程：殖民行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Tutorial: Colonizing a Planet

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:601`

## TUTORIAL_COLONIZATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_COLONIZATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S殖民§!界面是为殖民准备行星的地方。\n\n必须选择用哪个§S物种§!来定居到该行星上，指定§S殖民地规划类型§!可以设置其发展目标。\n\n这里还会总览可用的§S区划§!、§S行星规模§!以及可能的§S修正§!，此外还有可能影响到行星的§S行星特征§!。\n\n基于§S行星§!的自然资源优先发展可以帮你最大化利用殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/tutorial_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

The §SColonization§! screen is where you prepare a planet for colonization.\n\nA §SSpecies§! must be selected to settle the planet, and a §SColony Designation§! assigned to set its development focus.\n\nAn overview of available §SDistricts§!, §SPlanet Size§!, and potential §SModifiers§! is displayed, along with any §SPlanetary Features§! that may affect the planet.\n\nPrioritizing development based on the §SPlanet's§! natural resources will help you make the most of your colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/tutorial_l_english.yml:602`
